FILE: backend/app/application/authorization/authorization_service.py
####################################################################
     1: from __future__ import annotations
     2: 
     3: from app.domain.authorization.permissions import Permission, has_permission
     4: from app.domain.identity.principal import Principal
     5: 
     6: 
     7: class AuthenticationRequiredError(PermissionError):
     8:     """No valid authenticated principal exists."""
     9: 
    10: 
    11: class PermissionDeniedError(PermissionError):
    12:     """Authenticated principal lacks a permission."""
    13: 
    14: 
    15: class AuthorizationService:
    16:     """Provider-neutral authorization boundary."""
    17: 
    18:     def require_authenticated(
    19:         self,
    20:         principal: Principal | None,
    21:     ) -> Principal:
    22:         if principal is None or not principal.is_active:
    23:             raise AuthenticationRequiredError(
    24:                 "Authenticated application identity is required."
    25:             )
    26:         return principal
    27: 
    28:     def require_permission(
    29:         self,
    30:         *,
    31:         principal: Principal | None,
    32:         permission: Permission,
    33:     ) -> Principal:
    34:         authenticated = self.require_authenticated(principal)
    35: 
    36:         if not has_permission(
    37:             roles=authenticated.roles,
    38:             permission=permission,
    39:         ):
    40:             raise PermissionDeniedError(
    41:                 f"Permission required: {permission.value}."
    42:             )
    43: 
    44:         return authenticated
    45: 
    46: 
    47: 
    48: 

####################################################################
FILE: backend/app/domain/authorization/permissions.py
####################################################################
     1: from __future__ import annotations
     2: 
     3: from enum import StrEnum
     4: 
     5: 
     6: class Permission(StrEnum):
     7:     ANNOUNCEMENT_VIEW = "announcement.view"
     8:     ANNOUNCEMENT_PUBLISH = "announcement.publish"
     9:     FUNCTIONALITY_CONTROL = "functionality.control"
    10:     INTERNAL_DATA_READ = "internal_data.read"
    11: 
    12: 
    13: class Role(StrEnum):
    14:     BETA_USER = "beta_user"
    15:     OPERATOR = "operator"
    16:     ADMIN = "admin"
    17: 
    18: 
    19: ROLE_PERMISSIONS: dict[Role, frozenset[Permission]] = {
    20:     Role.BETA_USER: frozenset(),
    21:     Role.OPERATOR: frozenset(
    22:         {
    23:             Permission.ANNOUNCEMENT_VIEW,
    24:             Permission.ANNOUNCEMENT_PUBLISH,
    25:             Permission.FUNCTIONALITY_CONTROL,
    26:             Permission.INTERNAL_DATA_READ,
    27:         }
    28:     ),
    29:     Role.ADMIN: frozenset(
    30:         {
    31:             Permission.ANNOUNCEMENT_VIEW,
    32:             Permission.ANNOUNCEMENT_PUBLISH,
    33:             Permission.FUNCTIONALITY_CONTROL,
    34:             Permission.INTERNAL_DATA_READ,
    35:         }
    36:     ),
    37: }
    38: 
    39: 
    40: def has_permission(
    41:     *,
    42:     roles: frozenset[str],
    43:     permission: Permission,
    44: ) -> bool:
    45:     return any(
    46:         permission in ROLE_PERMISSIONS.get(
    47:             Role(raw_role),
    48:             frozenset(),
    49:         )
    50:         for raw_role in roles
    51:         if raw_role in {role.value for role in Role}
    52:     )

FILE: backend/app/domain/identity/principal.py
####################################################################
     1: from __future__ import annotations
     2: 
     3: from dataclasses import dataclass
     4: from enum import StrEnum
     5: 
     6: 
     7: class AccountStatus(StrEnum):
     8:     ACTIVE = "active"
     9:     SUSPENDED = "suspended"
    10:     DISABLED = "disabled"
    11: 
    12: 
    13: @dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
    14: class Principal:
    15:     """Authenticated application identity."""
    16: 
    17:     user_id: str
    18:     email: str
    19:     email_verified: bool
    20:     account_status: AccountStatus
    21:     roles: frozenset[str] = frozenset()
    22: 
    23:     @property
    24:     def is_active(self) -> bool:
    25:         return (
    26:             self.account_status == AccountStatus.ACTIVE
    27:             and self.email_verified
    28:         )

     1: from __future__ import annotations
     2: 
     3: from dataclasses import asdict
     4: from typing import Any
     5: from uuid import uuid4
     6: 
     7: from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
     8: from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
     9: 
    10: from app.application.announcements.template_editor_service import (
    11:     AnnouncementTemplateEditorService,
    12: )
    13: 
    14: 
    15: class PreviewRequest(BaseModel):
    16:     template_id: str
    17:     template_version: int | None = None
    18:     values: dict[str, str] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    19: 
    20: 
    21: class CreateDraftRequest(BaseModel):
    22:     publication_id: str | None = None
    23:     template_id: str
    24:     template_version: int | None = None
    25:     title: str
    26:     values: dict[str, str] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    27: 
    28: 
    29: def create_template_editor_router(
    30:     service: AnnouncementTemplateEditorService,
    31: ) -> APIRouter:
    32:     """Create the HTTP adapter for the template-driven editor.
    33: 
    34:     Composition owns the service. This router never creates repositories,
    35:     providers, Telegram clients, or publication executors.
    36:     """
    37: 
    38:     if service is None:
    39:         raise ValueError("service cannot be None.")
    40: 
    41:     router = APIRouter(
    42:         prefix="/api/announcement-operations/template-editor",
    43:         tags=["announcement-operations"],
    44:     )
    45: 
    46:     @router.get("/templates")
    47:     def list_templates() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    48:         return [asdict(item) for item in service.list_approved_templates()]
    49: 
    50:     @router.post("/preview")
    51:     def preview(request: PreviewRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
    52:         try:
    53:             result = service.preview(
    54:                 template_id=request.template_id,
    55:                 values=request.values,
    56:                 version=request.template_version,
    57:             )
    58:         except (LookupError, ValueError) as exc:
    59:             raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(exc)) from exc
    60: 
    61:         return asdict(result)
    62: 
    63:     @router.post("/drafts")
    64:     def create_draft(request: CreateDraftRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
    65:         try:
    66:             publication = service.create_draft(
    67:                 publication_id=request.publication_id or str(uuid4()),
    68:                 template_id=request.template_id,
    69:                 title=request.title,
    70:                 values=request.values,
    71:                 version=request.template_version,
    72:             )
    73:         except (LookupError, ValueError) as exc:
    74:             raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(exc)) from exc
    75: 
    76:         return {
    77:             "id": publication.publication_id,
    78:             "title": publication.title,
    79:             "content": publication.content,
    80:             "template_id": publication.template_id,
    81:             "template_version": publication.template_version,
    82:             "publication_type": publication.publication_type.value,
    83:             "status": publication.status.value,
    84:             "created_at": publication.created_at.isoformat()
    85:             if publication.created_at
    86:             else None,
    87:         }
    88: 
    89:     return router
    90:

FILE: backend/app/api/announcement_operations_approval.py
####################################################################
     1: from __future__ import annotations
     2: 
     3: from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable
     4: 
     5: from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
     6: from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
     7: 
     8: from app.application.announcements.approval.async_approval_service import (
     9:     AsyncAnnouncementPublicationApprovalService,
    10: )
    11: 
    12: 
    13: AdminAuthorizer = Callable[[], Awaitable[bool]]
    14: 
    15: 
    16: class PublicationApprovalRequest(BaseModel):
    17:     actor: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=255)
    18: 
    19: 
    20: class PublicationApprovalResponse(BaseModel):
    21:     publication_id: str
    22:     status: str
    23:     approved_at: str | None
    24: 
    25: 
    26: async def _default_admin_authorizer() -> bool:
    27:     return False
    28: 
    29: 
    30: def create_announcement_operations_approval_router(
    31:     *,
    32:     approval_service: AsyncAnnouncementPublicationApprovalService,
    33:     admin_authorizer: AdminAuthorizer = _default_admin_authorizer,
    34: ) -> APIRouter:
    35:     if approval_service is None:
    36:         raise ValueError("approval_service cannot be None.")
    37:     if admin_authorizer is None:
    38:         raise ValueError("admin_authorizer cannot be None.")
    39: 
    40:     router = APIRouter(
    41:         prefix="/api/announcement-operations",
    42:         tags=["announcement-operations"],
    43:     )
    44: 
    45:     async def require_admin() -> None:
    46:         try:
    47:             authorized = await admin_authorizer()
    48:         except Exception:
    49:             raise HTTPException(
    50:                 status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
    51:                 detail="Administrator authorization required.",
    52:             ) from None
    53: 
    54:         if not authorized:
    55:             raise HTTPException(
    56:                 status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
    57:                 detail="Administrator authorization required.",
    58:             )
    59: 
    60:     @router.post(
    61:         "/publications/{publication_id}/approve",
    62:         response_model=PublicationApprovalResponse,
    63:         status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
    64:         dependencies=[Depends(require_admin)],
    65:     )
    66:     async def approve_publication(
    67:         publication_id: str,
    68:         request: PublicationApprovalRequest,
    69:     ) -> PublicationApprovalResponse:
    70:         try:
    71:             result = await approval_service.approve(
    72:                 publication_id=publication_id,
    73:                 actor=request.actor,
    74:             )
    75:         except LookupError as exc:
    76:             raise HTTPException(
    77:                 status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
    78:                 detail=str(exc),
    79:             ) from exc
    80:         except ValueError as exc:
    81:             raise HTTPException(
    82:                 status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
    83:                 detail=str(exc),
    84:             ) from exc
    85: 
    86:         publication = result.publication
    87:         return PublicationApprovalResponse(
    88:             publication_id=publication.publication_id,
    89:             status=publication.status.value,
    90:             approved_at=(
    91:                 publication.approved_at.isoformat()
    92:                 if publication.approved_at is not None
    93:                 else None
    94:             ),
    95:         )
    96: 
    97:     return router
    98: 
    99: 
   100: __all__ = [
   101:     "AdminAuthorizer",
   102:     "PublicationApprovalRequest",
   103:     "PublicationApprovalResponse",
   104:     "create_announcement_operations_approval_router",
   105: ]

FILE: backend/app/api/announcement_controls.py
####################################################################
     1: from __future__ import annotations
     2: 
     3: from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable
     4: from os import getenv
     5: 
     6: from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
     7: from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
     8: 
     9: from app.application.announcement.announcement_composer import (
    10:     AnnouncementComposer,
    11: )
    12: from app.application.announcement.announcement_publisher import (
    13:     ControlledAnnouncementPublisher,
    14:     PublicationDisabledError,
    15: )
    16: from app.application.announcement.models import AnnouncementType
    17: 
    18: 
    19: class AnnouncementPublishRequest(BaseModel):
    20:     """Administrator request for publishing a Beta announcement."""
    21: 
    22:     announcement_type: AnnouncementType
    23:     title: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=120)
    24:     body: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=1800)
    25:     footer: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=300)
    26: 
    27: 
    28: class AnnouncementPublishResponse(BaseModel):
    29:     """Controlled publication result."""
    30: 
    31:     channel_id: str
    32:     message_id: str
    33:     announcement_type: str
    34:     published_at: str
    35: 
    36: 
    37: AdminAuthorizer = Callable[[], Awaitable[bool]]
    38: 
    39: 
    40: async def _default_admin_authorizer() -> bool:
    41:     """
    42:     Fail-closed default administrator authorization.
    43: 
    44:     Production composition must inject the real administrator
    45:     authorization mechanism.
    46:     """
    47:     return False
    48: 
    49: 
    50: def create_announcement_router(
    51:     *,
    52:     publisher: ControlledAnnouncementPublisher,
    53:     composer: AnnouncementComposer | None = None,
    54:     admin_authorizer: AdminAuthorizer = _default_admin_authorizer,
    55: ) -> APIRouter:
    56:     """
    57:     Create the WS-02 administrator announcement router.
    58: 
    59:     The API layer does not:
    60:     - talk directly to Telegram;
    61:     - construct Telegram clients;
    62:     - bypass AnnouncementPolicy;
    63:     - bypass ControlledAnnouncementPublisher;
    64:     - perform autonomous publication.
    65:     """
    66: 
    67:     if publisher is None:
    68:         raise ValueError("publisher cannot be None.")
    69: 
    70:     if admin_authorizer is None:
    71:         raise ValueError("admin_authorizer cannot be None.")
    72: 
    73:     announcement_composer = composer or AnnouncementComposer()
    74: 
    75:     router = APIRouter(
    76:         prefix="/admin/announcements",
    77:         tags=["admin-announcements"],
    78:     )
    79: 
    80:     async def require_admin() -> None:
    81:         try:
    82:             authorized = await admin_authorizer()
    83:         except Exception:
    84:             raise HTTPException(
    85:                 status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
    86:                 detail="Administrator authorization required.",
    87:             ) from None
    88: 
    89:         if not authorized:
    90:             raise HTTPException(
    91:                 status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
    92:                 detail="Administrator authorization required.",
    93:             )
    94: 
    95:     @router.post(
    96:         "/publish",
    97:         response_model=AnnouncementPublishResponse,
    98:         status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
    99:         dependencies=[Depends(require_admin)],
   100:     )
   101:     async def publish_announcement(
   102:         request: AnnouncementPublishRequest,
   103:     ) -> AnnouncementPublishResponse:
   104:         # ---------------------------------------------------------
   105:         # Composition / policy validation
   106:         # ---------------------------------------------------------
   107:         try:
   108:             composed = announcement_composer.compose(
   109:                 announcement_type=request.announcement_type,
   110:                 title=request.title,
   111:                 body=request.body,
   112:                 footer=request.footer,
   113:             )
   114:         except ValueError as exc:
   115:             raise HTTPException(
   116:                 status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
   117:                 detail=str(exc),
   118:             ) from exc
   119: 
   120:         # ---------------------------------------------------------
   121:         # Configuration
   122:         # ---------------------------------------------------------
   123:         try:
   124:             channel_id = _resolve_channel_id()
   125:         except ValueError as exc:
   126:             raise HTTPException(
   127:                 status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
   128:                 detail=str(exc),
   129:             ) from exc
   130: 
   131:         # ---------------------------------------------------------
   132:         # Controlled publication
   133:         #
   134:         # PublicationDisabledError is an expected operational state.
   135:         # It is translated here at the HTTP boundary into 503.
   136:         # ---------------------------------------------------------
   137:         try:
   138:             record = await publisher.publish(
   139:                 channel_id=channel_id,
   140:                 text=composed.rendered_text,
   141:                 announcement_type=(
   142:                     composed.announcement.announcement_type.value
   143:                 ),
   144:             )
   145:         except PublicationDisabledError:
   146:             raise HTTPException(
   147:                 status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
   148:                 detail="Telegram announcement publishing is disabled.",
   149:             ) from None
   150:         except ValueError as exc:
   151:             raise HTTPException(
   152:                 status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
   153:                 detail=str(exc),
   154:             ) from exc
   155: 
   156:         # ---------------------------------------------------------
   157:         # Successful publication
   158:         # ---------------------------------------------------------
   159:         return AnnouncementPublishResponse(
   160:             channel_id=record.channel_id,
   161:             message_id=record.message_id,
   162:             announcement_type=record.announcement_type,
   163:             published_at=record.published_at.isoformat(),
   164:         )
   165: 
   166:     return router
   167: 
   168: 
   169: def _resolve_channel_id() -> str:
   170:     """
   171:     Resolve the Beta 1 announcement channel from configuration.
   172: 
   173:     The channel ID is deliberately not hard-coded.
   174:     """
   175: 
   176:     channel_id = getenv(
   177:         "BETA1_ANNOUNCEMENT_CHANNEL_ID",
   178:     )
   179: 
   180:     if not channel_id:
   181:         raise ValueError(
   182:             "BETA1_ANNOUNCEMENT_CHANNEL_ID is not configured."
   183:         )
   184: 
   185:     return channel_id
   186: 
   187: 
   188: __all__ = [
   189:     "AnnouncementPublishRequest",
   190:     "AnnouncementPublishResponse",
   191:     "create_announcement_router",
   192: ]
   193:

FILE: backend/app/application/announcements/approval/composition.py
####################################################################
     1: from __future__ import annotations
     2: 
     3: from dataclasses import dataclass
     4: 
     5: from app.api.announcement_operations_approval import (
     6:     AdminAuthorizer,
     7:     create_announcement_operations_approval_router,
     8: )
     9: from app.application.announcements.approval.approval_service import (
    10:     AnnouncementPublicationApprovalService,
    11: )
    12: 
    13: 
    14: @dataclass(frozen=True)
    15: class AnnouncementApprovalComposition:
    16:     """Composition boundary for WS-23.1.3 approval."""
    17: 
    18:     service: AnnouncementPublicationApprovalService
    19:     router: object
    20: 
    21: 
    22: def create_announcement_approval_composition(
    23:     *,
    24:     approval_service: AnnouncementPublicationApprovalService,
    25:     admin_authorizer: AdminAuthorizer,
    26: ) -> AnnouncementApprovalComposition:
    27:     """
    28:     Compose the WS-23.1.3 approval boundary.
    29: 
    30:     The composition root owns dependency creation.
    31: 
    32:     This function:
    33:     - reuses the injected approval service;
    34:     - reuses the injected administrator authorizer;
    35:     - creates only the API router;
    36:     - does not create a repository;
    37:     - does not create a database/session;
    38:     - does not create a Telegram client;
    39:     - does not invoke WS-22 execution.
    40:     """
    41: 
    42:     if approval_service is None:
    43:         raise ValueError("approval_service cannot be None.")
    44: 
    45:     if admin_authorizer is None:
    46:         raise ValueError("admin_authorizer cannot be None.")
    47: 
    48:     router = create_announcement_operations_approval_router(
    49:         approval_service=approval_service,
    50:         admin_authorizer=admin_authorizer,
    51:     )
    52: 
    53:     return AnnouncementApprovalComposition(
    54:         service=approval_service,
    55:         router=router,
    56:     )
    57: 
    58: 
    59: __all__ = [
    60:     "AnnouncementApprovalComposition",
    61:     "create_announcement_approval_composition",
    62: ]

     2: 
     3: from dataclasses import dataclass

     6: from app.application.announcements.repositories import PublicationRepository

     8: from app.domain.announcements.publication import Publication
     9: from app.domain.announcements.template_models import PublicationStatus
    10: 
    11: 
    12: @dataclass(frozen=True)
    13: class PublicationPreview:
    14:     publication_type: str
    15:     template_id: str
    16:     template_version: int
    17:     content: str
    18: 
    19: 
    20: class AnnouncementPublicationService:
    21:     def __init__(
    22:         self,
    23:         *,
    24:         templates: AnnouncementTemplateRegistry,
    25:         publications: PublicationRepository,
    26:     ) -> None:
    27:         if templates is None or publications is None:
    28:             raise ValueError("templates and publications are required.")
    29:         self._templates = templates
    30:         self._publications = publications
    31: 
    32:     def preview(
    33:         self,
    34:         *,
    35:         template_id: str,
    36:         values: Mapping[str, str],
    37:         version: int | None = None,
    38:     ) -> PublicationPreview:
    39:         template = self._templates.get_version(template_id, version)
    40:         content = self._templates.preview(
    41:             template_id=template_id,
    42:             values=dict(values),
    43:             version=template.version,
    44:         )
    45:         return PublicationPreview(
    46:             publication_type=template.publication_type.value,
    47:             template_id=template.template_id,
    48:             template_version=template.version,
    49:             content=content,
    50:         )
    51: 
    52:     def create(
    53:         self,
    54:         *,
    55:         publication_id: str,
    56:         template_id: str,
    57:         title: str,
    58:         values: Mapping[str, str],
    59:         version: int | None = None,
    60:     ) -> Publication:
    61:         template = self._templates.get_version(template_id, version)
    62:         content = self._templates.preview(
    63:             template_id=template_id,
    64:             values=dict(values),
    65:             version=template.version,
    66:         )
    67:         publication = Publication(
    68:             publication_id=publication_id,
    69:             template_id=template.template_id,
    70:             template_version=template.version,
    71:             publication_type=template.publication_type,
    72:             title=title,
    73:             content=content,
    74:             status=PublicationStatus.DRAFT,
    75:         )
    76:         self._publications.save(publication)
    77:         return publication
    78: 
    79:     def _get(self, publication_id: str) -> Publication:
    80:         publication = self._publications.get(publication_id)
    81:         if publication is None:
    82:             raise LookupError(f"Unknown publication '{publication_id}'.")
    83:         return publication
    84: 
    85:     def request_approval(self, publication_id: str) -> Publication:
    86:         publication = self._get(publication_id)
    87:         publication.request_approval()
    88:         self._publications.save(publication)
    89:         return publication
    90: 
    91:     def approve(self, publication_id: str) -> Publication:
    92:         publication = self._get(publication_id)
    93:         publication.approve()
    94:         self._publications.save(publication)
    95:         return publication
    96: 
    97:     def begin_publish(self, publication_id: str) -> Publication:
    98:         publication = self._get(publication_id)
    99:         publication.begin_publish()
   100:         self._publications.save(publication)
   101:         return publication
   102: 
   103:     def mark_publish_failure(self, publication_id: str, reason: str) -> Publication:
   104:         publication = self._get(publication_id)
   105:         publication.mark_failure(reason)
   106:         self._publications.save(publication)
   107:         return publication
   108: 
   109:     def mark_publish_success(self, publication_id: str) -> Publication:
   110:         publication = self._get(publication_id)
   111:         publication.publish_success()
   112:         self._publications.save(publication)
   113:         return publication

    18: class AnnouncementTemplateEditorService:
    19:     """Application service for the template-driven publication editor.
    20: 
    21:     This service only prepares editor metadata, validates/preview-renders
    22:     template values, and creates drafts. It has no channel/provider dependency
    23:     and never performs external publication.
    24:     """
    25: 
    26:     def __init__(
    27:         self,
    28:         *,
    29:         templates: AnnouncementTemplateRegistry,
    30:         publications: AnnouncementPublicationService,
    31:     ) -> None:

    33:             raise ValueError("templates and publications are required.")

    36:

    41:             TemplateEditorOption(
    42:                 template_id=template.template_id,
    43:                 name=template.name,
    44:                 category=(
    45:                     template.category
    46:                     if template.category
    47:                     else template.publication_type.value
    48:                 ),
    49:                 description=template.description
    50:                 or "Approved announcement pattern.",
    51:                 version=template.active_version,
    52:                 publication_type=template.publication_type.value,
    53:                 fields=build_editor_fields(
    54:                     template.get_version().required_fields,
    55:                     template.get_version().optional_fields,
    56:                 ),
    57:             )
    58:             for template in template_models
    59:         )
    60: 
    61:     def preview(
    62:         self,
    63:         *,
    64:         template_id: str,
    65:         values: Mapping[str, str],
    66:         version: int | None = None,
    67:     ) -> TemplateEditorPreview:
    68:         result = self._publications.preview(
    69:             template_id=template_id,
    70:             values=values,
    71:             version=version,
    72:         )
    73:         return TemplateEditorPreview(
    74:             template_id=result.template_id,
    75:             template_version=result.template_version,
    76:             publication_type=result.publication_type,
    77:             content=result.content,
    78:         )

    90:             publication_id=publication_id,

    92:             title=title,
    93:             values=values,
    94:             version=version,

FILE: backend/app/infrastructure/announcements/sqlalchemy_publication_repository.py
####################################################################
     1: """PostgreSQL adapter for the existing PublicationRepository contract."""
     2: 
     3: from __future__ import annotations
     4: 
     5: from datetime import datetime, timezone
     6: 
     7: from sqlalchemy import DateTime, Integer, String, Text, select
     8: from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker
     9: from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column
    10: 
    11: from app.application.announcements.repositories import PublicationRepository
    12: from app.domain.announcements.publication import Publication
    13: from app.domain.announcements.template_models import (
    14:     PublicationStatus,
    15:     PublicationType,
    16: )
    17: from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import Base
    18: 
    19: 
    20: class PublicationModel(Base):
    21:     """Canonical durable announcement publication state."""
    22: 
    23:     __tablename__ = "announcement_publications"
    24: 
    25:     publication_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255), primary_key=True)
    26:     template_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=False)
    27:     template_version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False)
    28:     publication_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    29:     title: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), nullable=False)
    30:     content: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=False)
    31:     status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False, index=True)
    32:     created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)
    33:     approved_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    34:     published_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    35:     failure_reason: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text)
    36: 
    37: 
    38: class SqlAlchemyPublicationRepository(PublicationRepository):
    39:     """Async PostgreSQL adapter for the existing repository contract."""
    40: 
    41:     def __init__(
    42:         self,
    43:         session_factory: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession],
    44:     ) -> None:
    45:         if session_factory is None:
    46:             raise ValueError("session_factory cannot be None.")
    47:         self._session_factory = session_factory
    48: 
    49:     def _to_domain(self, model: PublicationModel) -> Publication:
    50:         return Publication(
    51:             publication_id=model.publication_id,
    52:             template_id=model.template_id,
    53:             template_version=model.template_version,
    54:             publication_type=PublicationType(model.publication_type),
    55:             title=model.title,
    56:             content=model.content,
    57:             status=PublicationStatus(model.status),
    58:             created_at=model.created_at,
    59:             approved_at=model.approved_at,
    60:             published_at=model.published_at,
    61:             failure_reason=model.failure_reason,
    62:         )
    63: 
    64:     def _from_domain(self, publication: Publication) -> PublicationModel:
    65:         return PublicationModel(
    66:             publication_id=publication.publication_id,
    67:             template_id=publication.template_id,
    68:             template_version=publication.template_version,
    69:             publication_type=publication.publication_type.value,
    70:             title=publication.title,
    71:             content=publication.content,
    72:             status=publication.status.value,
    73:             created_at=publication.created_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc),
    74:             approved_at=publication.approved_at,
    75:             published_at=publication.published_at,
    76:             failure_reason=publication.failure_reason,
    77:         )
    78: 
    79:     async def save_async(self, publication: Publication) -> None:
    80:         async with self._session_factory() as session:
    81:             model = await session.get(
    82:                 PublicationModel,
    83:                 publication.publication_id,
    84:             )
    85:             if model is None:
    86:                 session.add(self._from_domain(publication))
    87:             else:
    88:                 model.template_id = publication.template_id
    89:                 model.template_version = publication.template_version
    90:                 model.publication_type = publication.publication_type.value
    91:                 model.title = publication.title
    92:                 model.content = publication.content
    93:                 model.status = publication.status.value
    94:                 model.created_at = publication.created_at or model.created_at
    95:                 model.approved_at = publication.approved_at
    96:                 model.published_at = publication.published_at
    97:                 model.failure_reason = publication.failure_reason
    98:             await session.commit()
    99: 
   100:     async def get_async(self, publication_id: str) -> Publication | None:
   101:         async with self._session_factory() as session:
   102:             model = await session.get(PublicationModel, publication_id)
   103:             return self._to_domain(model) if model else None
   104: 
   105:     async def list_all_async(self) -> tuple[Publication, ...]:
   106:         async with self._session_factory() as session:
   107:             result = await session.execute(
   108:                 select(PublicationModel).order_by(PublicationModel.created_at)
   109:             )
   110:             return tuple(self._to_domain(model) for model in result.scalars().all())
   111: 
   112:     # The existing repository contract is synchronous. These methods make
   113:     # accidental async DB use explicit rather than silently blocking.
   114:     def save(self, publication: Publication) -> None:
   115:         raise RuntimeError(
   116:             "SqlAlchemyPublicationRepository requires its async boundary; "
   117:             "use save_async()."
   118:         )
   119: 
   120:     def get(self, publication_id: str) -> Publication | None:
   121:         raise RuntimeError(
   122:             "SqlAlchemyPublicationRepository requires its async boundary; "
   123:             "use get_async()."
   124:         )
   125: 
   126:     def list_all(self) -> tuple[Publication, ...]:
   127:         raise RuntimeError(
   128:             "SqlAlchemyPublicationRepository requires its async boundary; "
   129:             "use list_all_async()."
   130:         )
   131:

FILE: backend/app/application/announcements/approval/approval_service.py
####################################################################
     1: from __future__ import annotations
     2: 
     3: from dataclasses import dataclass
     4: from datetime import datetime, timezone
     5: from typing import Protocol
     6: 
     7: from app.application.announcements.publication_service import (
     8:     AnnouncementPublicationService,
     9: )
    10: from app.domain.announcements.publication import Publication
    11: 
    12: 
    13: class PublicationApprovalAudit(Protocol):
    14:     async def record_approval(
    15:         self,
    16:         *,
    17:         publication: Publication,
    18:         actor: str,
    19:         occurred_at: datetime,
    20:     ) -> None:
    21:         ...
    22: 
    23: 
    24: @dataclass(frozen=True)
    25: class PublicationApprovalResult:
    26:     publication: Publication
    27:     actor: str
    28:     approved_at: datetime
    29: 
    30: 
    31: class AnnouncementPublicationApprovalService:
    32:     """Authorized application boundary for WS-23.1.3 approval.
    33: 
    34:     Lifecycle validation remains in the Publication domain model.
    35:     Persistence remains behind PublicationRepository.
    36:     Telegram delivery is deliberately outside this service.
    37:     """
    38: 
    39:     def __init__(
    40:         self,
    41:         *,
    42:         publications: AnnouncementPublicationService,
    43:         audit: PublicationApprovalAudit,
    44:     ) -> None:
    45:         if publications is None or audit is None:
    46:             raise ValueError("publications and audit are required.")
    47:         self._publications = publications
    48:         self._audit = audit
    49: 
    50:     async def approve(
    51:         self,
    52:         *,
    53:         publication_id: str,
    54:         actor: str,
    55:     ) -> PublicationApprovalResult:
    56:         if not actor.strip():
    57:             raise ValueError("actor cannot be empty.")
    58: 
    59:         publication = self._publications.approve(publication_id)
    60:         approved_at = publication.approved_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    61: 
    62:         await self._audit.record_approval(
    63:             publication=publication,
    64:             actor=actor.strip(),
    65:             occurred_at=approved_at,
    66:         )
    67: 
    68:         return PublicationApprovalResult(
    69:             publication=publication,
    70:             actor=actor.strip(),
    71:             approved_at=approved_at,
    72:         )
    73:

FILE: backend/app/application/announcements/approval/async_approval_service.py
####################################################################
     1: from __future__ import annotations
     2: 
     3: from dataclasses import dataclass
     4: from datetime import datetime, timezone
     5: from typing import Protocol
     6: 
     7: from app.domain.announcements.publication import Publication
     8: from app.domain.announcements.template_models import PublicationStatus
     9: 
    10: 
    11: class AsyncPublicationStore(Protocol):
    12:     async def get_async(self, publication_id: str) -> Publication | None:
    13:         ...
    14: 
    15:     async def save_async(self, publication: Publication) -> None:
    16:         ...
    17: 
    18: 
    19: class PublicationApprovalAudit(Protocol):
    20:     async def record_approval(
    21:         self,
    22:         *,
    23:         publication: Publication,
    24:         actor: str,
    25:         occurred_at: datetime,
    26:     ) -> None:
    27:         ...
    28: 
    29: 
    30: @dataclass(frozen=True)
    31: class AsyncPublicationApprovalResult:
    32:     publication: Publication
    33:     actor: str
    34:     approved_at: datetime
    35: 
    36: 
    37: class AsyncAnnouncementPublicationApprovalService:
    38:     """Async PostgreSQL-backed approval application boundary.
    39: 
    40:     The domain model remains the authority for lifecycle transitions.
    41:     This service never invokes WS-22 execution or Telegram.
    42:     """
    43: 
    44:     def __init__(
    45:         self,
    46:         *,
    47:         publications: AsyncPublicationStore,
    48:         audit: PublicationApprovalAudit,
    49:     ) -> None:
    50:         if publications is None or audit is None:
    51:             raise ValueError("publications and audit are required.")
    52:         self._publications = publications
    53:         self._audit = audit
    54: 
    55:     async def approve(
    56:         self,
    57:         *,
    58:         publication_id: str,
    59:         actor: str,
    60:     ) -> AsyncPublicationApprovalResult:
    61:         if not actor.strip():
    62:             raise ValueError("actor cannot be empty.")
    63: 
    64:         publication = await self._publications.get_async(publication_id)
    65:         if publication is None:
    66:             raise LookupError(f"Unknown publication '{publication_id}'.")
    67: 
    68:         if publication.status is not PublicationStatus.PENDING_APPROVAL:
    69:             # Use the domain transition so the canonical lifecycle error remains
    70:             # authoritative and consistent with the existing domain model.
    71:             publication.approve()
    72: 
    73:         else:
    74:             publication.approve()
    75: 
    76:         approved_at = publication.approved_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    77: 
    78:         await self._publications.save_async(publication)
    79:         await self._audit.record_approval(
    80:             publication=publication,
    81:             actor=actor.strip(),
    82:             occurred_at=approved_at,
    83:         )
    84: 
    85:         return AsyncPublicationApprovalResult(
    86:             publication=publication,
    87:             actor=actor.strip(),
    88:             approved_at=approved_at,
    89:         )
    90: 
    91: 
    92: __all__ = [
    93:     "AsyncAnnouncementPublicationApprovalService",
    94:     "AsyncPublicationApprovalResult",
    95: ]
    96:

####################################################################
FILE: backend/app/application/announcements/execution/approved_publication_execution.py
####################################################################
     1: from __future__ import annotations
     2: 
     3: from dataclasses import dataclass
     4: from uuid import UUID
     5: 
     6: from app.application.announcements.execution.contracts import (
     7:     PublicationExecutionRequest,
     8:     PublicationExecutionResult,
     9: )
    10: from app.application.announcements.execution.durable_execution_service import (
    11:     DurablePublicationExecutionService,
    12: )
    13: from app.domain.announcements.publication import Publication
    14: 
    15: 
    16: @dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
    17: class ApprovedPublicationExecutionService:
    18:     """Application boundary from WS-23 approved state into WS-22."""
    19: 
    20:     durable_execution: DurablePublicationExecutionService
    21: 
    22:     def __post_init__(self) -> None:
    23:         if self.durable_execution is None:
    24:             raise ValueError("durable_execution cannot be None.")
    25: 
    26:     async def execute(
    27:         self,
    28:         *,
    29:         publication: Publication,
    30:         actor: str,
    31:         idempotency_key: str,
    32:         correlation_id: str,
    33:     ) -> PublicationExecutionResult:
    34:         """Delegate an approved publication to the existing WS-22 service.
    35: 
    36:         WS-22 remains responsible for:
    37:         - durable execution;
    38:         - idempotency;
    39:         - execution persistence;
    40:         - execution audit;
    41:         - retry semantics;
    42:         - controlled provider invocation.
    43:         """
    44: 
    45:         if publication is None:
    46:             raise ValueError("publication cannot be None.")
    47: 
    48:         if not actor.strip():
    49:             raise ValueError("actor cannot be empty.")
    50: 
    51:         if not idempotency_key.strip():
    52:             raise ValueError("idempotency_key cannot be empty.")
    53: 
    54:         if not correlation_id.strip():
    55:             raise ValueError("correlation_id cannot be empty.")
    56: 
    57:         try:
    58:             publication_uuid = UUID(publication.publication_id)
    59:         except (ValueError, AttributeError) as exc:
    60:             raise ValueError(
    61:                 "Approved publication ID must be a UUID for WS-22 durable execution."
    62:             ) from exc
    63: 
    64:         request = PublicationExecutionRequest(
    65:             publication_id=publication_uuid,
    66:             idempotency_key=idempotency_key.strip(),
    67:             correlation_id=correlation_id.strip(),
    68:             actor=actor.strip(),
    69:         )
    70: 
    71:         return await self.durable_execution.execute(
    72:             publication=_PublicationExecutionAdapter(publication),
    73:             request=request,
    74:         )
    75: 
    76: 
    77: class _PublicationExecutionAdapter:
    78:     """Adapt the WS-23 publication to the existing WS-22 execution contract."""
    79: 
    80:     def __init__(self, publication: Publication) -> None:
    81:         self._publication = publication
    82: 
    83:     @property
    84:     def id(self) -> UUID:
    85:         return UUID(self._publication.publication_id)
    86: 
    87:     @property
    88:     def title(self) -> str:
    89:         return self._publication.title
    90: 
    91:     @property
    92:     def content(self) -> str:
    93:         return self._publication.content
    94: 
    95:     @property
    96:     def status(self):
    97:         return self._publication.status
    98: 
    99:     def begin_publish(self) -> None:
   100:         self._publication.begin_publish()
   101: 
   102:     def mark_failure(self, reason: str) -> None:
   103:         self._publication.mark_failure(reason)
   104: 
   105:     def publish_success(self, provider_message_id: str | None = None) -> None:
   106:         del provider_message_id
   107:         self._publication.publish_success()
   108: 
   109: 
   110: __all__ = ["ApprovedPublicationExecutionService"]

####################################################################
FILE: backend/app/application/announcements/execution/durable_execution_service.py
####################################################################
     1: """WS-22.3.2 durable execution orchestration."""
     2: 
     3: from __future__ import annotations
     4: 
     5: from app.application.announcements.execution.contracts import (
     6:     AnnouncementPublisher,
     7:     PublicationExecutionRequest,
     8:     PublicationExecutionResult,
     9:     PublicationExecutionStatus,
    10: )
    11: from app.application.announcements.execution.durable_contracts import (
    12:     DurableExecutionStatus,
    13:     PublicationExecutionRepository,
    14: )
    15: 
    16: 
    17: class DurablePublicationExecutionService:
    18:     """Retry-safe execution service backed by durable idempotency state.
    19: 
    20:     A completed idempotency key is never sent again.
    21: 
    22:     A STARTED record is treated as an interrupted attempt and is retryable.
    23: 
    24:     A FAILED record is also retryable. Before a retry is executed, the
    25:     repository is responsible for incrementing the durable attempt counter
    26:     and transitioning the record back to STARTED.
    27: 
    28:     The repository implementation must make creation atomic against a
    29:     unique idempotency key.
    30:     """
    31: 
    32:     def __init__(
    33:         self,
    34:         *,
    35:         publisher: AnnouncementPublisher,
    36:         execution_repository: PublicationExecutionRepository,
    37:         audit,
    38:     ) -> None:
    39:         if publisher is None:
    40:             raise ValueError("publisher cannot be None.")
    41: 
    42:         if execution_repository is None:
    43:             raise ValueError("execution_repository cannot be None.")
    44: 
    45:         if audit is None:
    46:             raise ValueError("audit cannot be None.")
    47: 
    48:         self._publisher = publisher
    49:         self._repository = execution_repository
    50:         self._audit = audit
    51: 
    52:     async def execute(
    53:         self,
    54:         *,
    55:         publication,
    56:         request: PublicationExecutionRequest,
    57:     ) -> PublicationExecutionResult:
    58:         existing = await self._repository.get_by_idempotency_key(
    59:             request.idempotency_key
    60:         )
    61: 
    62:         # A successful execution is terminal for the idempotency key.
    63:         # Never call the external provider again.
    64:         if (
    65:             existing is not None
    66:             and existing.status is DurableExecutionStatus.SUCCEEDED
    67:         ):
    68:             return PublicationExecutionResult(
    69:                 publication_id=existing.publication_id,
    70:                 status=PublicationExecutionStatus.IDEMPOTENT_REPLAY,
    71:                 idempotency_key=existing.idempotency_key,
    72:                 correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
    73:                 provider_message_id=existing.provider_message_id,
    74:             )
    75: 
    76:         self._assert_approved(publication)
    77: 
    78:         # The repository owns the atomic unique-key operation. If another
    79:         # worker already created the same key, re-read and honour its state.
    80:         if existing is None:
    81:             try:
    82:                 record = await self._repository.create_started(
    83:                     publication_id=publication.id,
    84:                     idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
    85:                     correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
    86:                 )
    87:             except Exception:
    88:                 record = await self._repository.get_by_idempotency_key(
    89:                     request.idempotency_key
    90:                 )
    91: 
    92:                 if record is None:
    93:                     raise
    94: 
    95:         elif existing.status is DurableExecutionStatus.FAILED:
    96:             # A FAILED execution is retryable. Reuse the same durable
    97:             # idempotency record and increment its attempt number rather
    98:             # than creating a second row.
    99:             record = await self._repository.start_retry(
   100:                 idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
   101:             )
   102: 
   103:         else:
   104:             # STARTED represents an interrupted/incomplete attempt.
   105:             # Preserve the existing durable attempt number and retry it.
   106:             record = existing
   107: 
   108:         publication.begin_publish()
   109: 
   110:         try:
   111:             provider_message_id = await self._publisher.publish(
   112:                 publication_id=publication.id,
   113:                 title=publication.title,
   114:                 content=publication.content,
   115:                 correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
   116:                 idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
   117:             )
   118:         except Exception as exc:
   119:             error_code = self._error_code(exc)
   120: 
   121:             publication.mark_failure(error_code)
   122: 
   123:             await self._repository.mark_failed(
   124:                 idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
   125:                 error_code=error_code,
   126:             )
   127: 
   128:             await self._audit.record_failure(
   129:                 publication_id=publication.id,
   130:                 actor=request.actor,
   131:                 correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
   132:                 error_code=error_code,
   133:             )
   134: 
   135:             return PublicationExecutionResult(
   136:                 publication_id=publication.id,
   137:                 status=PublicationExecutionStatus.FAILED,
   138:                 idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
   139:                 correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
   140:                 error_code=error_code,
   141:             )
   142: 
   143:         publication.publish_success(provider_message_id)
   144: 
   145:         await self._repository.mark_succeeded(
   146:             idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
   147:             provider_message_id=provider_message_id,
   148:         )
   149: 
   150:         await self._audit.record_success(
   151:             publication_id=publication.id,
   152:             actor=request.actor,
   153:             correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
   154:             provider_message_id=provider_message_id,
   155:         )
   156: 
   157:         return PublicationExecutionResult(
   158:             publication_id=publication.id,
   159:             status=PublicationExecutionStatus.SUCCEEDED,
   160:             idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
   161:             correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
   162:             provider_message_id=provider_message_id,
   163:         )
   164: 
   165:     @staticmethod
   166:     def _assert_approved(publication) -> None:
   167:         value = getattr(publication.status, "value", publication.status)
   168: 
   169:         if value != "approved":
   170:             raise ValueError(
   171:                 "Only approved publications may be executed."
   172:             )
   173: 
   174:     @staticmethod
   175:     def _error_code(exc: Exception) -> str:
   176:         explicit = getattr(exc, "error_code", None)
   177: 
   178:         return (
   179:             explicit.strip()[:100]
   180:             if isinstance(explicit, str) and explicit.strip()
   181:             else type(exc).__name__.upper()[:100]
   182:         )

FILE: backend/app/application/announcements/execution/durable_execution_composition.py
####################################################################
     1: """WS-22.3.5.3 canonical durable publication execution composition.
     2: 
     3: This module is the composition boundary for the WS-22 durable execution
     4: vertical slice.
     5: 
     6: It deliberately does not create a new persistence architecture. The
     7: canonical PostgreSQL engine and async session factory are supplied by the
     8: platform persistence boundary in:
     9: 
    10:     app.infrastructure.persistence.database
    11: 
    12: The resulting object graph is:
    13: 
    14:     PostgreSQL
    15:         |
    16:         v
    17:     canonical async session factory
    18:         |
    19:         +--> execution repository
    20:         |
    21:         +--> audit repository
    22:                   |
    23:                   v
    24:         DurablePublicationExecutionAudit
    25:                   |
    26:                   v
    27:         DurablePublicationExecutionService
    28: 
    29: Provider selection remains outside this module.
    30: """
    31: 
    32: from __future__ import annotations
    33: 
    34: from dataclasses import dataclass
    35: 
    36: from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncEngine, AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker
    37: 
    38: from app.application.announcements.execution.audit_adapter import (
    39:     DurablePublicationExecutionAudit,
    40: )
    41: from app.application.announcements.execution.durable_execution_service import (
    42:     DurablePublicationExecutionService,
    43: )
    44: from app.infrastructure.announcements.publication_audit_repository import (
    45:     SqlAlchemyPublicationAuditRepository,
    46: )
    47: from app.infrastructure.announcements.sqlalchemy_publication_execution_repository import (
    48:     SqlAlchemyPublicationExecutionRepository,
    49: )
    50: from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import (
    51:     create_async_database_engine,
    52:     create_async_session_factory,
    53: )
    54: 
    55: 
    56: @dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
    57: class DurablePublicationExecutionComposition:
    58:     """Fully composed WS-22 durable execution boundary."""
    59: 
    60:     engine: AsyncEngine
    61:     session_factory: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession]
    62:     execution_repository: SqlAlchemyPublicationExecutionRepository
    63:     audit_repository: SqlAlchemyPublicationAuditRepository
    64:     audit: DurablePublicationExecutionAudit
    65:     service: DurablePublicationExecutionService
    66: 
    67:     async def dispose(self) -> None:
    68:         """Release the shared PostgreSQL async engine."""
    69: 
    70:         await self.engine.dispose()
    71: 
    72: 
    73: def create_durable_publication_execution_composition(
    74:     *,
    75:     publisher,
    76: ) -> DurablePublicationExecutionComposition:
    77:     """Create the canonical WS-22 durable execution graph.
    78: 
    79:     The publisher is deliberately injected because provider selection belongs
    80:     outside the durable execution composition.
    81: 
    82:     This function creates exactly one PostgreSQL engine and exactly one async
    83:     session factory. Both durable repositories receive that same factory.
    84:     """
    85: 
    86:     if publisher is None:
    87:         raise ValueError("publisher cannot be None.")
    88: 
    89:     engine = create_async_database_engine()
    90:     session_factory = create_async_session_factory(engine)
    91: 
    92:     execution_repository = SqlAlchemyPublicationExecutionRepository(
    93:         session_factory
    94:     )
    95: 
    96:     audit_repository = SqlAlchemyPublicationAuditRepository(
    97:         session_factory
    98:     )
    99: 
   100:     audit = DurablePublicationExecutionAudit(
   101:         repository=audit_repository,
   102:     )
   103: 
   104:     service = DurablePublicationExecutionService(
   105:         publisher=publisher,
   106:         execution_repository=execution_repository,
   107:         audit=audit,
   108:     )
   109: 
   110:     return DurablePublicationExecutionComposition(
   111:         engine=engine,
   112:         session_factory=session_factory,
   113:         execution_repository=execution_repository,
   114:         audit_repository=audit_repository,
   115:         audit=audit,
   116:         service=service,
   117:     )
   118: 
   119: 
   120: __all__ = [
   121:     "DurablePublicationExecutionComposition",
   122:     "create_durable_publication_execution_composition",
   123: ]

####################################################################
FILE: backend/app/application/announcements/execution/legacy_publisher_bridge.py
####################################################################
     1: """WS-22.3.3.x legacy publisher migration bridge.
     2: 
     3: This module provides the explicit migration boundary between the existing
     4: Beta announcement publisher contract and the canonical WS-22 execution
     5: publisher contract.
     6: 
     7: The bridge is intentionally provider-independent. It does not know about
     8: Telegram, HTTP, credentials, or external SDKs.
     9: """
    10: 
    11: from __future__ import annotations
    12: 
    13: from typing import Protocol
    14: from uuid import UUID
    15: 
    16: from app.application.announcements.execution.contracts import (
    17:     AnnouncementPublisher,
    18: )
    19: 
    20: 
    21: class LegacyAnnouncementPublisher(Protocol):
    22:     """Existing Beta publisher contract.
    23: 
    24:     This protocol mirrors the legacy application boundary that is still used
    25:     by the existing ControlledAnnouncementPublisher path.
    26: 
    27:     It is intentionally isolated here as a migration dependency and must not
    28:     be used by the new WS-22 execution service.
    29:     """
    30: 
    31:     async def publish(
    32:         self,
    33:         *,
    34:         channel_id: str,
    35:         text: str,
    36:     ) -> str:
    37:         """Publish using the existing legacy publication boundary."""
    38: 
    39: 
    40: class LegacyAnnouncementPublisherBridge(AnnouncementPublisher):
    41:     """Adapt the existing publisher to the canonical WS-22 port.
    42: 
    43:     The bridge is the temporary migration boundary between:
    44: 
    45:         WS-22 execution contract
    46:                     â†“
    47:         legacy announcement publisher
    48:                     â†“
    49:         existing provider adapter
    50: 
    51:     No provider-specific behaviour belongs here.
    52: 
    53:     The channel identifier is configuration supplied by the composition root.
    54:     It is deliberately not persisted or invented by the execution service.
    55:     """
    56: 
    57:     def __init__(
    58:         self,
    59:         *,
    60:         publisher: LegacyAnnouncementPublisher,
    61:         channel_id: str,
    62:     ) -> None:
    63:         if publisher is None:
    64:             raise ValueError("publisher cannot be None.")
    65: 
    66:         if not channel_id.strip():
    67:             raise ValueError("channel_id cannot be empty.")
    68: 
    69:         self._publisher = publisher
    70:         self._channel_id = channel_id
    71: 
    72:     async def publish(
    73:         self,
    74:         *,
    75:         publication_id: UUID,
    76:         title: str,
    77:         content: str,
    78:         correlation_id: str,
    79:         idempotency_key: str,
    80:     ) -> str | None:
    81:         """Publish through the existing legacy publisher.
    82: 
    83:         WS-22 execution metadata is deliberately not passed to the legacy
    84:         publisher because that older contract does not support it.
    85: 
    86:         Idempotency and correlation remain owned by the WS-22 execution
    87:         service and durable execution repository.
    88:         """
    89: 
    90:         del publication_id
    91:         del correlation_id
    92:         del idempotency_key
    93: 
    94:         text = self._compose_message(
    95:             title=title,
    96:             content=content,
    97:         )
    98: 
    99:         return await self._publisher.publish(
   100:             channel_id=self._channel_id,
   101:             text=text,
   102:         )
   103: 
   104:     @staticmethod
   105:     def _compose_message(
   106:         *,
   107:         title: str,
   108:         content: str,
   109:     ) -> str:
   110:         normalized_title = title.strip()
   111:         normalized_content = content.strip()
   112: 
   113:         if not normalized_title:
   114:             raise ValueError("title cannot be empty.")
   115: 
   116:         if not normalized_content:
   117:             raise ValueError("content cannot be empty.")
   118: 
   119:         return f"{normalized_title}\n\n{normalized_content}"
   120: 
   121: 
   122: __all__ = [
   123:     "LegacyAnnouncementPublisher",
   124:     "LegacyAnnouncementPublisherBridge",
   125: ]

####################################################################
FILE: backend/app/infrastructure/announcements/execution/telegram_publication_publisher_adapter.py
####################################################################
     1: """
     2: WS-23.1.4 Telegram publication adapter.
     3: 
     4: Architecture:
     5: 
     6: WS-22 AnnouncementPublisher
     7:         â†“
     8: TelegramPublicationPublisherAdapter
     9:         â†“
    10: ControlledAnnouncementPublisher
    11:         â†“
    12: TelegramAnnouncementPublisher
    13:         â†“
    14: Existing Telegram client
    15: 
    16: This adapter:
    17: - does not create a Telegram client;
    18: - does not implement idempotency;
    19: - does not create a repository;
    20: - does not create a database;
    21: - does not bypass the controlled publisher;
    22: - translates the provider-independent WS-22 contract into the
    23:   existing controlled Telegram publication boundary.
    24: """
    25: 
    26: from __future__ import annotations
    27: 
    28: from os import getenv
    29: from uuid import UUID
    30: 
    31: from app.application.announcement.announcement_publisher import (
    32:     ControlledAnnouncementPublisher,
    33: )
    34: from app.application.announcements.execution.contracts import (
    35:     AnnouncementPublisher,
    36: )
    37: 
    38: 
    39: class TelegramPublicationPublisherAdapter(AnnouncementPublisher):
    40:     """Bridge the canonical WS-22 publisher contract to Telegram."""
    41: 
    42:     def __init__(
    43:         self,
    44:         *,
    45:         publisher: ControlledAnnouncementPublisher,
    46:         channel_id: str | None = None,
    47:         announcement_type: str = "beta_update",
    48:     ) -> None:
    49:         if publisher is None:
    50:             raise ValueError("publisher cannot be None.")
    51: 
    52:         resolved_channel_id = channel_id or getenv(
    53:             "BETA1_ANNOUNCEMENT_CHANNEL_ID"
    54:         )
    55: 
    56:         if not resolved_channel_id or not resolved_channel_id.strip():
    57:             raise ValueError(
    58:                 "BETA1_ANNOUNCEMENT_CHANNEL_ID is not configured."
    59:             )
    60: 
    61:         if not announcement_type.strip():
    62:             raise ValueError(
    63:                 "announcement_type cannot be empty."
    64:             )
    65: 
    66:         self._publisher = publisher
    67:         self._channel_id = resolved_channel_id.strip()
    68:         self._announcement_type = announcement_type.strip()
    69: 
    70:     async def publish(
    71:         self,
    72:         *,
    73:         publication_id: UUID,
    74:         title: str,
    75:         content: str,
    76:         correlation_id: str,
    77:         idempotency_key: str,
    78:     ) -> str | None:
    79:         """
    80:         Publish through the existing controlled Telegram boundary.
    81: 
    82:         WS-22 remains responsible for:
    83:         - idempotency;
    84:         - execution persistence;
    85:         - execution audit;
    86:         - retry semantics.
    87: 
    88:         This adapter only translates the publication request.
    89:         """
    90: 
    91:         if not isinstance(publication_id, UUID):
    92:             raise ValueError(
    93:                 "publication_id must be a UUID."
    94:             )
    95: 
    96:         if not title.strip():
    97:             raise ValueError(
    98:                 "title cannot be empty."
    99:             )
   100: 
   101:         if not content.strip():
   102:             raise ValueError(
   103:                 "content cannot be empty."
   104:             )
   105: 
   106:         if not correlation_id.strip():
   107:             raise ValueError(
   108:                 "correlation_id cannot be empty."
   109:             )
   110: 
   111:         if not idempotency_key.strip():
   112:             raise ValueError(
   113:                 "idempotency_key cannot be empty."
   114:             )
   115: 
   116:         text = (
   117:             f"*{title.strip()}*\n\n"
   118:             f"{content.strip()}"
   119:         )
   120: 
   121:         record = await self._publisher.publish(
   122:             channel_id=self._channel_id,
   123:             text=text,
   124:             announcement_type=self._announcement_type,
   125:         )
   126: 
   127:         return record.message_id
   128: 
   129: 
   130: __all__ = [
   131:     "TelegramPublicationPublisherAdapter",
   132: ]

####################################################################
FILE: backend/app/infrastructure/telegram/telegram_announcement_client.py
####################################################################
     1: from __future__ import annotations
     2: 
     3: import os
     4: from typing import Any
     5: 
     6: from telegram import Bot
     7: 
     8: 
     9: class TelegramAnnouncementClient:
    10:     """
    11:     Outbound-only Telegram client for controlled announcements.
    12: 
    13:     This client is completely separate from TelegramBotRuntime.
    14: 
    15:     Responsibilities:
    16:     - resolve TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN;
    17:     - create an outbound Telegram Bot client lazily;
    18:     - send controlled announcement messages;
    19:     - release client resources.
    20: 
    21:     This client does NOT:
    22:     - poll Telegram updates;
    23:     - register handlers;
    24:     - start polling;
    25:     - execute classroom conversations;
    26:     - perform administrator authorization;
    27:     - bypass ControlledAnnouncementPublisher.
    28:     """
    29: 
    30:     def __init__(
    31:         self,
    32:         *,
    33:         token: str | None = None,
    34:     ) -> None:
    35:         self._token = (
    36:             token
    37:             or os.getenv("TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN")
    38:         )
    39: 
    40:         if not self._token:
    41:             raise RuntimeError(
    42:                 "TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN is missing."
    43:             )
    44: 
    45:         self._bot: Bot | None = None
    46: 
    47:     def _get_bot(self) -> Bot:
    48:         """
    49:         Return the outbound-only Telegram Bot client.
    50: 
    51:         The Bot is created lazily and never starts polling.
    52:         """
    53: 
    54:         if self._bot is None:
    55:             self._bot = Bot(
    56:                 token=self._token,
    57:             )
    58: 
    59:         return self._bot
    60: 
    61:     async def send_message(
    62:         self,
    63:         *,
    64:         chat_id: str,
    65:         text: str,
    66:         parse_mode: str | None = None,
    67:     ) -> Any:
    68:         """
    69:         Send one outbound Telegram message.
    70: 
    71:         No Telegram polling is performed.
    72:         """
    73: 
    74:         bot = self._get_bot()
    75: 
    76:         return await bot.send_message(
    77:             chat_id=chat_id,
    78:             text=text,
    79:             parse_mode=parse_mode,
    80:         )
    81: 
    82:     async def close(self) -> None:
    83:         """
    84:         Release the outbound Telegram client.
    85:         """
    86: 
    87:         if self._bot is None:
    88:             return
    89: 
    90:         await self._bot.shutdown()
    91: 
    92:         self._bot = None
    93: 
    94: 
    95: __all__ = [
    96:     "TelegramAnnouncementClient",
    97: ]

   167: def _required_environment(name: str) -> str:
   168:     value = os.getenv(name, "").strip()
   169: 
   170:     if not value:
   171:         raise RuntimeError(
   172:             f"{name} is required for authentication email composition."
   173:         )
   174: 
   175:     return value
   176: 
   177: 
   178: def _create_announcement_components():
   179:     telegram_client = TelegramAnnouncementClient()
   180: 
   181:     announcement_publisher = TelegramAnnouncementPublisher(
   182:         telegram_client=telegram_client,
   183:     )
   184: 
   185:     return (
   186:         telegram_client,
   187:         announcement_publisher,
   188:     )
   189: 
   190: 
   191: @asynccontextmanager
   192: async def lifespan(
   193:     application: FastAPI,
   194: ):
   195:     scheduler_runtime = application.state.scheduler_runtime
   196: 
   197:     try:
   198:         await scheduler_runtime.start()
   199:     except Exception as exc:
   200:         print(
   201:             "Cybersecurity scheduler startup failed:",
   202:             exc,
   203:         )
   204: 
   205:     yield
   206: 
   207:     try:
   208:         await scheduler_runtime.stop()
   209:     except Exception as exc:
   210:         print(
   211:             "Cybersecurity scheduler shutdown failed:",
   212:             exc,
   213:         )
   214:     finally:
   215:         telegram_client = getattr(
   216:             application.state,
   217:             "announcement_telegram_client",
   218:             None,
   219:         )
   220: 
   221:         if telegram_client is not None:
   222:             try:
   223:                 await telegram_client.close()
   224:             except Exception as exc:
   225:                 print(
   226:                     "Telegram announcement client shutdown failed:",
   227:                     exc,
   228:                 )
   229: 
   230:         auth_engine = getattr(
   231:             application.state,
   232:             "auth_engine",
   233:             None,
   234:         )
   235: 
   236:         if auth_engine is not None:
   237:             try:
   238:                 await auth_engine.dispose()
   239:             except Exception as exc:
   240:                 print(
   241:                     "PostgreSQL authentication engine shutdown failed:",
   242:                     exc,
   243:                 )
   244: 
   245:         provider_usage_engine = getattr(
   246:             application.state,
   247:             "provider_usage_engine",
   248:             None,
   249:         )
   250: 
   251:         if provider_usage_engine is not None:
   252:             try:
   253:                 provider_usage_engine.dispose()
   254:             except Exception as exc:
   255:                 print(
   256:                     "PostgreSQL provider-usage engine shutdown failed:",
   257:                     exc,
   258:                 )
   259: 
   260:

FILE: frontend/app/components/operations-center/announcement-operations/TemplateDrivenPublicationEditor.tsx
