     1: "use client";
     2: 
     3: import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
     4: import {
     5:   createTemplateDraft,
     6:   getEditorTemplates,
     7:   previewTemplate,
     8:   type TemplateEditorOption,
     9: } from "@/app/lib/api/announcementTemplateEditor";
    10: 
    11: export default function TemplateDrivenPublicationEditor() {
    12:   const [templates, setTemplates] = useState<TemplateEditorOption[]>([]);
    13:   const [templateId, setTemplateId] = useState("");
    14:   const [values, setValues] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
    15:   const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
    16:   const [preview, setPreview] = useState("");
    17:   const [loadingTemplates, setLoadingTemplates] = useState(true);
    18:   const [previewing, setPreviewing] = useState(false);
    19:   const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    20:   const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    21:   const [message, setMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
    22: 
    23:   const selectedTemplate = useMemo(
    24:     () => templates.find((item) => item.template_id === templateId) ?? null,
    25:     [templateId, templates],
    26:   );
    27: 
    28:   useEffect(() => {
    29:     getEditorTemplates()
    30:       .then((items) => {
    31:         setTemplates(items);
    32:         if (items.length > 0) {
    33:           setTemplateId(items[0].template_id);
    34:         }
    35:       })
    36:       .catch((reason: unknown) => {
    37:         setError(
    38:           reason instanceof Error
    39:             ? reason.message
    40:             : "Announcement types could not be loaded.",
    41:         );
    42:       })
    43:       .finally(() => setLoadingTemplates(false));
    44:   }, []);
    45: 
    46:   useEffect(() => {
    47:     if (!selectedTemplate) {
    48:       setValues({});
    49:       setPreview("");
    50:       return;
    51:     }
    52: 
    53:     const nextValues: Record<string, string> = {};
    54:     for (const field of selectedTemplate.fields) {
    55:       nextValues[field.name] = "";
    56:     }
    57: 
    58:     setValues(nextValues);
    59:     setPreview("");
    60:     setError(null);
    61:   }, [selectedTemplate]);
    62: 
    63:   const requiredFieldsMissing = selectedTemplate
    64:     ? selectedTemplate.fields.some(
    65:         (field) => field.required && !values[field.name]?.trim(),
    66:       )
    67:     : true;
    68: 
    69:   const canPreview =
    70:     Boolean(selectedTemplate) &&
    71:     Boolean(title.trim()) &&
    72:     !requiredFieldsMissing;
    73: 
    74:   function updateValue(name: string, value: string) {
    75:     setValues((current) => ({ ...current, [name]: value }));
    76:     setMessage(null);
    77:   }
    78: 
    79:   async function handlePreview() {
    80:     if (!selectedTemplate || !canPreview) {
    81:       return;
    82:     }
    83: 
    84:     setPreviewing(true);
    85:     setError(null);
    86:     setMessage(null);
    87: 
    88:     try {
    89:       const result = await previewTemplate({
    90:         templateId: selectedTemplate.template_id,
    91:         templateVersion: selectedTemplate.version,
    92:         values,
    93:       });
    94:       setPreview(result.content);
    95:     } catch (reason: unknown) {
    96:       setError(
    97:         reason instanceof Error
    98:           ? reason.message
    99:           : "Preview could not be generated.",
   100:       );
   101:     } finally {
   102:       setPreviewing(false);
   103:     }
   104:   }
   105: 
   106:   async function handleSaveDraft() {
   107:     if (!selectedTemplate || !canPreview) {
   108:       return;
   109:     }
   110: 
   111:     setSaving(true);
   112:     setError(null);
   113:     setMessage(null);
   114: 
   115:     try {
   116:       const result = await createTemplateDraft({
   117:         templateId: selectedTemplate.template_id,
   118:         templateVersion: selectedTemplate.version,
   119:         title: title.trim(),
   120:         values,
   121:       });
   122: 
   123:       setMessage(
   124:         `Draft ${result.id} saved from ${result.template_id} v${result.template_version}.`,
   125:       );
   126:     } catch (reason: unknown) {
   127:       setError(
   128:         reason instanceof Error
   129:           ? reason.message
   130:           : "The draft could not be saved.",
   131:       );
   132:     } finally {
   133:       setSaving(false);
   134:     }
   135:   }
   136: 
   137:   return (
   138:     <main className="min-h-full p-6 lg:p-10">
   139:       <div className="mb-8">
   140:         <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-fuchsia-300">
   141:           Announcement Operations
   142:         </p>
   143:         <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-white">
   144:           Create publication
   145:         </h1>
   146:         <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-400">
   147:           Select an approved announcement type, complete its controlled fields,
   148:           preview it, and save a draft.
   149:         </p>
   150:       </div>
   151: 
   152:       {error && (
   153:         <div
   154:           role="alert"
   155:           className="mb-6 rounded-2xl border border-rose-500/30 bg-rose-500/10 p-4 text-sm text-rose-200"
   156:         >
   157:           {error}
   158:         </div>
   159:       )}
   160: 
   161:       {message && (
   162:         <div className="mb-6 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10 p-4 text-sm text-emerald-200">
   163:           {message}
   164:         </div>
   165:       )}
   166: 
   167:       <div className="grid gap-6 xl:grid-cols-2">
   168:         <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950/70 p-6">
   169:           <h2 className="font-semibold text-white">Publication content</h2>
   170: 
   171:           <label
   172:             htmlFor="announcement-type"
   173:             className="mt-5 block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500"
   174:           >
   175:             Announcement type
   176:           </label>
   177: 
   178:           <select
   179:             id="announcement-type"
   180:             aria-label="Announcement type"
   181:             value={templateId}
   182:             disabled={loadingTemplates || templates.length === 0}
   183:             onChange={(event) => setTemplateId(event.target.value)}
   184:             className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white outline-none focus:border-fuchsia-500 disabled:opacity-50"
   185:           >
   186:             {loadingTemplates && (
   187:               <option value="">Loading announcement typesâ€¦</option>
   188:             )}
   189:             {!loadingTemplates && templates.length === 0 && (
   190:               <option value="">No announcement types available</option>
   191:             )}
   192:             {templates.map((template) => (
   193:               <option key={template.template_id} value={template.template_id}>
   194:                 {template.name}
   195:               </option>
   196:             ))}
   197:           </select>
   198: 
   199:           {selectedTemplate && (
   200:             <div className="mt-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/60 p-4">
   201:               <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
   202:                 <span className="rounded-full bg-fuchsia-500/10 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-fuchsia-300">
   203:                   {selectedTemplate.category}
   204:                 </span>
   205:                 <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">
   206:                   Approved version {selectedTemplate.version}
   207:                 </span>
   208:               </div>
   209:               <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-400">
   210:                 {selectedTemplate.description}
   211:               </p>
   212:             </div>
   213:           )}
   214: 
   215:           <label
   216:             htmlFor="announcement-title"
   217:             className="mt-5 block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500"
   218:           >
   219:             Publication title
   220:           </label>
   221:           <input
   222:             id="announcement-title"
   223:             value={title}
   224:             onChange={(event) => setTitle(event.target.value)}
   225:             className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white outline-none focus:border-fuchsia-500"
   226:             placeholder="Announcement title"
   227:           />
   228: 
   229:           {selectedTemplate?.fields.map((field) => (
   230:             <div key={field.name}>
   231:               <label
   232:                 htmlFor={`template-field-${field.name}`}
   233:                 className="mt-5 block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500"
   234:               >
   235:                 {field.label}
   236:                 {field.required && (
   237:                   <span className="ml-1 text-fuchsia-300">*</span>
   238:                 )}
   239:               </label>
   240: 
   241:               {field.multiline ? (
   242:                 <textarea
   243:                   id={`template-field-${field.name}`}
   244:                   value={values[field.name] ?? ""}
   245:                   onChange={(event) =>
   246:                     updateValue(field.name, event.target.value)
   247:                   }
   248:                   rows={5}
   249:                   className="mt-2 w-full resize-y rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white outline-none focus:border-fuchsia-500"
   250:                 />
   251:               ) : (
   252:                 <input
   253:                   id={`template-field-${field.name}`}
   254:                   value={values[field.name] ?? ""}
   255:                   onChange={(event) =>
   256:                     updateValue(field.name, event.target.value)
   257:                   }
   258:                   className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white outline-none focus:border-fuchsia-500"
   259:                 />
   260:               )}
   261:             </div>
   262:           ))}
   263: 
   264:           {selectedTemplate && requiredFieldsMissing && (
   265:             <p className="mt-4 text-xs text-amber-300">
   266:               Complete all required fields before previewing or saving.
   267:             </p>
   268:           )}
   269: 
   270:           <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
   271:             <button
   272:               type="button"
   273:               disabled={!canPreview || previewing}
   274:               onClick={handlePreview}
   275:               className="rounded-xl border border-fuchsia-500/50 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-fuchsia-200 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
   276:             >
   277:               {previewing ? "Previewingâ€¦" : "Preview"}
   278:             </button>
   279: 
   280:             <button
   281:               type="button"
   282:               disabled={!canPreview || saving}
   283:               onClick={handleSaveDraft}
   284:               className="rounded-xl bg-fuchsia-500 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
   285:             >
   286:               {saving ? "Savingâ€¦" : "Save draft"}
   287:             </button>
   288:           </div>
   289:         </section>
   290: 
   291:         <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950/70 p-6">
   292:           <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-fuchsia-300">
   293:             Preview
   294:           </p>

####################################################################
FILE: frontend/app/lib/api/announcementTemplateEditor.ts
####################################################################
     1: export interface TemplateEditorField {
     2:   name: string;
     3:   required: boolean;
     4:   label: string;
     5:   multiline: boolean;
     6: }
     7: 
     8: export interface TemplateEditorOption {
     9:   template_id: string;
    10:   name: string;
    11:   category: string;
    12:   description: string;
    13:   version: number;
    14:   publication_type: string;
    15:   fields: TemplateEditorField[];
    16: }
    17: 
    18: export interface TemplateEditorPreview {
    19:   template_id: string;
    20:   template_version: number;
    21:   publication_type: string;
    22:   content: string;
    23: }
    24: 
    25: export interface CreatedDraftPublication {
    26:   id: string;
    27:   title: string;
    28:   content: string;
    29:   template_id: string;
    30:   template_version: number;
    31:   publication_type: string;
    32:   status: string;
    33:   created_at: string | null;
    34: }
    35: 
    36: const API_BASE =
    37:   process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "http://localhost:8000";
    38: 
    39: async function request<T>(
    40:   path: string,
    41:   init?: RequestInit,
    42: ): Promise<T> {
    43:   const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${path}`, {
    44:     ...init,
    45:     headers: {
    46:       Accept: "application/json",
    47:       "Content-Type": "application/json",
    48:       ...(init?.headers ?? {}),
    49:     },
    50:     cache: "no-store",
    51:   });
    52: 
    53:   if (!response.ok) {
    54:     let detail = `Announcement template API returned ${response.status}.`;
    55: 
    56:     try {
    57:       const body = (await response.json()) as { detail?: string };
    58:       if (body.detail) {
    59:         detail = body.detail;
    60:       }
    61:     } catch {
    62:       // Preserve the stable HTTP error for non-JSON responses.
    63:     }
    64: 
    65:     throw new Error(detail);
    66:   }
    67: 
    68:   return response.json() as Promise<T>;
    69: }
    70: 
    71: export function getEditorTemplates(): Promise<TemplateEditorOption[]> {
    72:   return request<TemplateEditorOption[]>(
    73:     "/api/announcement-operations/template-editor/templates",
    74:   );
    75: }
    76: 
    77: export function previewTemplate(input: {
    78:   templateId: string;
    79:   templateVersion: number;
    80:   values: Record<string, string>;
    81: }): Promise<TemplateEditorPreview> {
    82:   return request<TemplateEditorPreview>(
    83:     "/api/announcement-operations/template-editor/preview",
    84:     {
    85:       method: "POST",
    86:       body: JSON.stringify({
    87:         template_id: input.templateId,
    88:         template_version: input.templateVersion,
    89:         values: input.values,
    90:       }),
    91:     },
    92:   );
    93: }
    94: 
    95: export function createTemplateDraft(input: {
    96:   templateId: string;
    97:   templateVersion: number;
    98:   title: string;
    99:   values: Record<string, string>;
   100: }): Promise<CreatedDraftPublication> {
   101:   return request<CreatedDraftPublication>(
   102:     "/api/announcement-operations/template-editor/drafts",
   103:     {
   104:       method: "POST",
   105:       body: JSON.stringify({
   106:         template_id: input.templateId,
   107:         template_version: input.templateVersion,
   108:         title: input.title,
   109:         values: input.values,
   110:       }),
   111:     },
   112:   );
   113: }

     1: from datetime import datetime, timezone
     2: from uuid import uuid4
     3: 
     4: import pytest
     5: 
     6: from app.application.announcements.execution.approved_publication_execution import (
     7:     ApprovedPublicationExecutionService,

    17: 
    18: 
    19: class FakeDurableExecution:
    20:     def __init__(self) -> None:
    21:         self.calls = []
    22: 
    23:     async def execute(self, *, publication, request):
    24:         self.calls.append((publication, request))
    25:         return type(
    26:             "Result",
    27:             (),
    28:             {
    29:                 "status": PublicationExecutionStatus.SUCCEEDED,
    30:             },
    31:         )()
    32: 
    33: 
    34: def make_publication(
    35:     *,
    36:     status: PublicationStatus = PublicationStatus.APPROVED,
    37: ) -> Publication:
    38:     return Publication(
    39:         publication_id=str(uuid4()),
    40:         template_id="beta-update",
    41:         template_version=1,
    42:         publication_type=PublicationType.BETA_UPDATE,
    43:         title="Beta update",
    44:         content="Test announcement",
    45:         status=status,
    46:         created_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
    47:     )
    48: 
    49: 
    50: @pytest.mark.asyncio
    51: async def test_approved_publication_delegates_to_existing_ws22_service() -> None:
    52:     durable = FakeDurableExecution()
    53:     service = ApprovedPublicationExecutionService(
    54:         durable_execution=durable,
    55:     )
    56: 
    57:     publication = make_publication()
    58: 
    59:     result = await service.execute(
    60:         publication=publication,
    61:         actor="admin-test",
    62:         idempotency_key="ws23-1-4-test-key",
    63:         correlation_id="ws23-1-4-correlation",
    64:     )
    65: 
    66:     assert result.status is PublicationExecutionStatus.SUCCEEDED
    67:     assert len(durable.calls) == 1
    68:     _, request = durable.calls[0]
    69:     assert request.actor == "admin-test"
    70:     assert request.idempotency_key == "ws23-1-4-test-key"
    71:     assert request.correlation_id == "ws23-1-4-correlation"
    72: 
    73: 
    74: @pytest.mark.asyncio
    75: async def test_non_uuid_publication_id_is_rejected_at_ws22_boundary() -> None:
    76:     durable = FakeDurableExecution()
    77:     service = ApprovedPublicationExecutionService(
    78:         durable_execution=durable,
    79:     )
    80: 
    81:     publication = Publication(
    82:         publication_id="ws23-publication-001",
    83:         template_id="beta-update",
    84:         template_version=1,
    85:         publication_type=PublicationType.BETA_UPDATE,
    86:         title="Beta update",
    87:         content="Test announcement",
    88:         status=PublicationStatus.APPROVED,
    89:     )
    90: 
    91:     with pytest.raises(
    92:         ValueError,
    93:         match="Approved publication ID must be a UUID",
    94:     ):
    95:         await service.execute(
    96:             publication=publication,
    97:             actor="admin-test",
    98:             idempotency_key="ws23-1-4-test-key",
    99:             correlation_id="ws23-1-4-correlation",
   100:         )
   101: 
   102:     assert durable.calls == []
   103: 
   104: 
   105: @pytest.mark.asyncio
   106: async def test_non_approved_publication_is_not_sent_directly() -> None:
   107:     durable = FakeDurableExecution()
   108:     service = ApprovedPublicationExecutionService(
   109:         durable_execution=durable,
   110:     )
   111: 
   112:     publication = make_publication(
   113:         status=PublicationStatus.PENDING_APPROVAL,
   114:     )
   115: 
   116:     # The integration boundary itself does not bypass WS-22. The canonical
   117:     # durable service remains responsible for enforcing approved-only execution.
   118:     class RejectingDurableExecution:
   119:         async def execute(self, *, publication, request):
   120:             raise ValueError("Only approved publications may be executed.")
   121: 
   122:     service = ApprovedPublicationExecutionService(
   123:         durable_execution=RejectingDurableExecution(),
   124:     )
   125: 
   126:     with pytest.raises(
   127:         ValueError,
   128:         match="Only approved publications may be executed",
   129:     ):
   130:         await service.execute(
   131:             publication=publication,
   132:             actor="admin-test",
   133:             idempotency_key="ws23-1-4-test-key",
   134:             correlation_id="ws23-1-4-correlation",
   135:         )
   136:

     1: from __future__ import annotations
     2: 
     3: from dataclasses import dataclass
     4: from uuid import uuid4
     5: 
     6: import pytest
     7: 
     8: from app.application.announcements.execution.contracts import (
     9:     PublicationExecutionRequest,
    10:     PublicationExecutionStatus,
    11: )
    12: from app.application.announcements.execution.durable_contracts import (
    13:     DurableExecutionStatus,
    14: )
    15: from app.application.announcements.execution.durable_execution_service import (
    16:     DurablePublicationExecutionService,
    17: )
    18: from app.domain.announcements.publication_status import PublicationStatus
    19: 
    20: 
    21: @dataclass
    22: class Publication:
    23:     id: object
    24:     status: PublicationStatus = PublicationStatus.APPROVED
    25:     title: str = "Beta 2"
    26:     content: str = "Release"
    27: 
    28:     def begin_publish(self):
    29:         self.status = PublicationStatus.PUBLISHING
    30: 
    31:     def publish_success(self, provider_message_id=None):
    32:         self.status = PublicationStatus.PUBLISHED
    33: 
    34:     def mark_failure(self, reason):
    35:         self.status = PublicationStatus.FAILED
    36: 
    37: 
    38: @dataclass
    39: class Record:
    40:     publication_id: object
    41:     idempotency_key: str
    42:     correlation_id: str
    43:     attempt: int
    44:     status: DurableExecutionStatus
    45:     provider_message_id: str | None = None
    46:     error_code: str | None = None
    47: 
    48: 
    49: class DurableFakeRepository:
    50:     """Persists state in a shared object, simulating a restart."""
    51: 
    52:     def __init__(self, store=None):
    53:         self.store = store if store is not None else {}
    54: 
    55:     async def get_by_idempotency_key(self, key):
    56:         return self.store.get(key)
    57: 
    58:     async def create_started(self, *, publication_id, idempotency_key, correlation_id):
    59:         if idempotency_key in self.store:
    60:             raise RuntimeError("duplicate idempotency key")
    61:         record = Record(
    62:             publication_id=publication_id,
    63:             idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
    64:             correlation_id=correlation_id,
    65:             attempt=1,
    66:             status=DurableExecutionStatus.STARTED,
    67:         )
    68:         self.store[idempotency_key] = record
    69:         return record
    70: 
    71:     async def mark_succeeded(self, *, idempotency_key, provider_message_id):
    72:         record = self.store[idempotency_key]
    73:         record.status = DurableExecutionStatus.SUCCEEDED
    74:         record.provider_message_id = provider_message_id
    75:         return record
    76: 
    77:     async def mark_failed(self, *, idempotency_key, error_code):
    78:         record = self.store[idempotency_key]
    79:         record.status = DurableExecutionStatus.FAILED
    80:         record.error_code = error_code
    81:         return record
    82: 
    83: 
    84: class Publisher:
    85:     def __init__(self):
    86:         self.calls = 0
    87: 
    88:     async def publish(self, **kwargs):
    89:         self.calls += 1
    90:         return "telegram-independent-provider-id"
    91: 
    92: 
    93: class Audit:
    94:     def __init__(self):
    95:         self.success = []
    96:         self.failure = []
    97: 
    98:     async def record_success(self, **kwargs):
    99:         self.success.append(kwargs)
   100: 
   101:     async def record_failure(self, **kwargs):
   102:         self.failure.append(kwargs)
   103: 
   104: 
   105: def req(pid):
   106:     return PublicationExecutionRequest(
   107:         publication_id=pid,
   108:         idempotency_key="durable-001",
   109:         actor="admin",
   110:         correlation_id="corr-001",
   111:     )
   112: 
   113: 
   114: @pytest.mark.asyncio
   115: async def test_duplicate_is_detected_after_service_restart():
   116:     store = {}
   117:     publisher = Publisher()
   118:     audit = Audit()
   119:     publication = Publication(uuid4())
   120: 
   121:     service_one = DurablePublicationExecutionService(
   122:         publisher=publisher,
   123:         execution_repository=DurableFakeRepository(store),
   124:         audit=audit,
   125:     )
   126:     first = await service_one.execute(
   127:         publication=publication,
   128:         request=req(publication.id),
   129:     )
   130: 
   131:     # New service instance: no in-memory idempotency state survives here.
   132:     service_two = DurablePublicationExecutionService(
   133:         publisher=publisher,
   134:         execution_repository=DurableFakeRepository(store),
   135:         audit=audit,
   136:     )
   137:     second = await service_two.execute(
   138:         publication=publication,
   139:         request=req(publication.id),
   140:     )
   141: 
   142:     assert first.status is PublicationExecutionStatus.SUCCEEDED
   143:     assert second.status is PublicationExecutionStatus.IDEMPOTENT_REPLAY
   144:     assert publisher.calls == 1
   145: 
   146: 
   147: @pytest.mark.asyncio
   148: async def test_success_is_durably_recorded():
   149:     store = {}
   150:     publisher = Publisher()
   151:     audit = Audit()
   152:     publication = Publication(uuid4())
   153: 
   154:     service = DurablePublicationExecutionService(
   155:         publisher=publisher,
   156:         execution_repository=DurableFakeRepository(store),
   157:         audit=audit,
   158:     )
   159: 
   160:     await service.execute(publication=publication, request=req(publication.id))
   161: 
   162:     record = store["durable-001"]
   163:     assert record.status is DurableExecutionStatus.SUCCEEDED
   164:     assert record.provider_message_id == "telegram-independent-provider-id"
   165:     assert record.correlation_id == "corr-001"
   166: 
   167: 
   168: @pytest.mark.asyncio
   169: async def test_failed_attempt_is_durably_recorded_without_sensitive_error_text():
   170:     class FailingPublisher:
   171:         async def publish(self, **kwargs):
   172:             raise RuntimeError("secret provider response: APIKEY=DO_NOT_STORE")
   173: 
   174:     store = {}
   175:     audit = Audit()
   176:     publication = Publication(uuid4())
   177: 
   178:     service = DurablePublicationExecutionService(
   179:         publisher=FailingPublisher(),
   180:         execution_repository=DurableFakeRepository(store),
   181:         audit=audit,
   182:     )
   183: 
   184:     result = await service.execute(
   185:         publication=publication,
   186:         request=req(publication.id),
   187:     )
   188: 
   189:     assert result.status is PublicationExecutionStatus.FAILED
   190:     record = store["durable-001"]
   191:     assert record.status is DurableExecutionStatus.FAILED
   192:     assert record.error_code == "RUNTIMEERROR"
   193:     assert "APIKEY" not in str(record)

    23: @dataclass
    24: class FakeTelegramMessage:
    25:     message_id: int
    26: 
    27: 
    28: class FakeTelegramClient:
    29:     """Deterministic Telegram transport test double."""
    30: 
    31:     def __init__(self) -> None:
    32:         self.calls: list[dict[str, str]] = []
    33: 
    34:     async def send_message(
    35:         self,
    36:         *,
    37:         chat_id: str,
    38:         text: str,
    39:         parse_mode: str,
    40:     ) -> FakeTelegramMessage:
    41:         self.calls.append(
    42:             {
    43:                 "chat_id": chat_id,
    44:                 "text": text,
    45:                 "parse_mode": parse_mode,
    46:             }
    47:         )
    48: 
    49:         return FakeTelegramMessage(message_id=9001)

    51: 
    52: class InMemoryExecutionRepository:
    53:     """Minimal durable repository double for the integration test."""
    54: 
    55:     def __init__(self) -> None:
    56:         self.records = {}
    57: 
    58:     async def get_by_idempotency_key(self, idempotency_key: str):
    59:         return self.records.get(idempotency_key)
    60: 
    61:     async def create_started(
    62:         self,
    63:         *,
    64:         publication_id,
    65:         idempotency_key,
    66:         correlation_id,
    67:     ):
    68:         from datetime import datetime, timezone
    69: 
    70:         from app.application.announcements.execution.durable_contracts import (
    71:             DurableExecutionStatus,
    72:             PublicationExecutionRecord,
    73:         )
    74: 
    75:         existing = self.records.get(idempotency_key)
    76: 
    77:         if existing is not None:
    78:             return existing
    79: 
    80:         now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    81: 
    82:         record = PublicationExecutionRecord(
    83:             publication_id=publication_id,
    84:             idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
    85:             correlation_id=correlation_id,
    86:             attempt=1,
    87:             status=DurableExecutionStatus.STARTED,
    88:             provider_message_id=None,
    89:             error_code=None,
    90:             created_at=now,
    91:             updated_at=now,
    92:         )
    93: 
    94:         self.records[idempotency_key] = record
    95:         return record
    96: 
    97:     async def mark_succeeded(
    98:         self,
    99:         *,
   100:         idempotency_key,
   101:         provider_message_id,
   102:     ):
   103:         from dataclasses import replace
   104:         from datetime import datetime, timezone
   105: 
   106:         from app.application.announcements.execution.durable_contracts import (
   107:             DurableExecutionStatus,
   108:         )
   109: 
   110:         record = self.records[idempotency_key]
   111: 
   112:         updated = replace(
   113:             record,
   114:             status=DurableExecutionStatus.SUCCEEDED,
   115:             provider_message_id=provider_message_id,
   116:             error_code=None,
   117:             updated_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
   118:         )
   119: 
   120:         self.records[idempotency_key] = updated
   121:         return updated
   122: 
   123:     async def mark_failed(
   124:         self,
   125:         *,
   126:         idempotency_key,
   127:         error_code,
   128:     ):
   129:         from dataclasses import replace
   130:         from datetime import datetime, timezone
   131: 
   132:         from app.application.announcements.execution.durable_contracts import (
   133:             DurableExecutionStatus,
   134:         )
   135: 
   136:         record = self.records[idempotency_key]
   137: 
   138:         updated = replace(
   139:             record,
   140:             status=DurableExecutionStatus.FAILED,
   141:             error_code=error_code,
   142:             updated_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
   143:         )
   144: 
   145:         self.records[idempotency_key] = updated
   146:         return updated
   147: 
   148: 
   149: class RecordingAudit:
   150:     def __init__(self) -> None:
   151:         self.successes: list[dict] = []
   152:         self.failures: list[dict] = []
   153: 
   154:     async def record_success(self, **kwargs) -> None:
   155:         self.successes.append(kwargs)
   156: 
   157:     async def record_failure(self, **kwargs) -> None:
   158:         self.failures.append(kwargs)
   159: 
   160: 
   161: @dataclass
   162: class ApprovedPublication:
   163:     id: object
   164:     title: str = "Beta 2"
   165:     content: str = "EasyChatBot Beta 2 is available."
   166:     status: str = "approved"
   167: 
   168:     def begin_publish(self) -> None:
   169:         pass
   170: 
   171:     def publish_success(
   172:         self,
   173:         provider_message_id: str | None = None,
   174:     ) -> None:
   175:         self.status = "published"
   176: 
   177:     def mark_failure(self, error_code: str) -> None:
   178:         self.status = "failed"
   179:

   181: @pytest.mark.asyncio
   182: async def test_ws22_3_3_executes_through_telegram_adapter() -> None:
   183:     telegram_client = FakeTelegramClient()
   184: 
   185:     telegram_publisher = TelegramAnnouncementPublisher(
   186:         telegram_client=telegram_client,
   187:     )
   188: 
   189:     bridge = LegacyAnnouncementPublisherBridge(
   190:         publisher=telegram_publisher,
   191:         channel_id="-100123456789",
   192:     )
   193: 
   194:     repository = InMemoryExecutionRepository()
   195:     audit = RecordingAudit()
   196: 
   197:     service = DurablePublicationExecutionService(
   198:         publisher=bridge,
   199:         execution_repository=repository,
   200:         audit=audit,
   201:     )
   202: 
   203:     publication_id = uuid4()
   204: 
   205:     request = PublicationExecutionRequest(
   206:         publication_id=publication_id,
   207:         idempotency_key=f"ws22-3-3-{uuid4()}",
   208:         actor="integration-test",
   209:         correlation_id=f"corr-{uuid4()}",
   210:     )
   211: 
   212:     publication = ApprovedPublication(
   213:         id=publication_id,
   214:     )
   215: 
   216:     result = await service.execute(
   217:         publication=publication,
   218:         request=request,
   219:     )
   220: 
   221:     assert result.status is PublicationExecutionStatus.SUCCEEDED
   222:     assert result.provider_message_id == "9001"
   223: 
   224:     assert len(telegram_client.calls) == 1
   225:     assert telegram_client.calls[0] == {
   226:         "chat_id": "-100123456789",
   227:         "text": (
   228:             "Beta 2\n\n"
   229:             "EasyChatBot Beta 2 is available."
   230:         ),
   231:         "parse_mode": "Markdown",
   232:     }
   233: 
   234:     persisted = await repository.get_by_idempotency_key(
   235:         request.idempotency_key
   236:     )
   237: 
   238:     assert persisted is not None
   239:     assert persisted.provider_message_id == "9001"
   240:     assert persisted.correlation_id == request.correlation_id
   241:     assert persisted.publication_id == publication_id
   242: 
   243:     assert len(audit.successes) == 1
   244:     assert audit.successes[0]["publication_id"] == publication_id
   245:     assert audit.successes[0]["actor"] == "integration-test"
   246:     assert (
   247:         audit.successes[0]["correlation_id"]
   248:         == request.correlation_id
   249:     )
   250:     assert (
   251:         audit.successes[0]["provider_message_id"]
   252:         == "9001"
   253:     )
   254: 
   255: 
   256: @pytest.mark.asyncio
   257: async def test_ws22_3_3_idempotency_prevents_second_telegram_send() -> None:
   258:     telegram_client = FakeTelegramClient()
   259: 
   260:     telegram_publisher = TelegramAnnouncementPublisher(
   261:         telegram_client=telegram_client,
   262:     )
   263: 
   264:     bridge = LegacyAnnouncementPublisherBridge(
   265:         publisher=telegram_publisher,
   266:         channel_id="-100123456789",
   267:     )
   268: 
   269:     repository = InMemoryExecutionRepository()
   270:     audit = RecordingAudit()
   271: 
   272:     service = DurablePublicationExecutionService(
   273:         publisher=bridge,
   274:         execution_repository=repository,
   275:         audit=audit,
   276:     )
   277: 
   278:     publication_id = uuid4()
   279: 
   280:     request = PublicationExecutionRequest(
   281:         publication_id=publication_id,
   282:         idempotency_key=f"ws22-3-3-idempotent-{uuid4()}",
   283:         actor="integration-test",
   284:         correlation_id=f"corr-{uuid4()}",
   285:     )
   286: 
   287:     first = await service.execute(
   288:         publication=ApprovedPublication(id=publication_id),
   289:         request=request,
   290:     )
   291: 
   292:     second = await service.execute(
   293:         publication=ApprovedPublication(id=publication_id),
   294:         request=request,
   295:     )
   296: 
   297:     assert first.status is PublicationExecutionStatus.SUCCEEDED
   298: 
   299:     assert (
   300:         second.status
   301:         is PublicationExecutionStatus.IDEMPOTENT_REPLAY
   302:     )
   303: 
   304:     assert second.provider_message_id == "9001"
   305: 
   306:     # Critical WS-22 guarantee:
   307:     # the provider receives exactly one publication.
   308:     assert len(telegram_client.calls) == 1
   309: 
   310:     # Only the original successful execution records an audit success.
   311:     assert len(audit.successes) == 1

    33:         chat_id: str,

    50: def create_test_app(
    51:     *,
    52:     client: FakeTelegramClient,
    53:     authorized: bool = True,
    54:     publishing_enabled: bool = True,
    55: ) -> FastAPI:
    56:     publisher = ControlledAnnouncementPublisher(

    61:     )

    64:         return authorized

    75:     return app

    78: class _TelegramPublisherDouble:
    79:     """Small test double implementing AnnouncementPublisher."""

    82:         self,

    86:         self._client = client
    87: 
    88:     async def publish(
    89:         self,
    90:         *,
    91:         channel_id: str,
    92:         text: str,
    93:     ) -> str:
    94:         result = await self._client.send_message(
    95:             chat_id=channel_id,
    96:             text=text,
    97:             parse_mode="Markdown",
    98:         )
    99: 
   100:         return str(result.message_id)
   101:

11. ALTERNATE APPLICATION ENTRYPOINTS

####################################################################
FILE: backend/api/main.py
####################################################################
     1: """
     2: EasyChatBot API Application.
     3: 
     4: Hosts the user interaction layer.
     5: 
     6: The API application is responsible only for:
     7: 
     8: - creating the FastAPI application;
     9: - registering API routes;
    10: - exposing the application health endpoint;
    11: - triggering application composition bootstrap during startup;
    12: - allowing the local frontend to communicate with the API.
    13: 
    14: Business logic belongs to application services.
    15: 
    16: Agent execution belongs to the agent/runtime layer.
    17: 
    18: Provider construction belongs to the composition layer.
    19: """
    20: 
    21: from __future__ import annotations
    22: 
    23: from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
    24: 
    25: from fastapi import FastAPI
    26: from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
    27: 
    28: from api.routes import router
    29: from api.character_theme_routes import (
    30:     router as character_theme_router,
    31: )
    32: from api.character_routes import (
    33:     router as character_router,
    34: )
    35: from api.cinema_agent import (
    36:     router as cinema_router,
    37: )
    38: 
    39: from application.bootstrap import (
    40:     bootstrap_application,
    41: )
    42: 
    43: from application.telegram_bootstrap import (
    44:     start_telegram_runtime,
    45:     stop_telegram_runtime,
    46: )
    47: 
    48: from app.application.provider_management.functionality_control_composition import (
    49:     create_standalone_functionality_control_service,
    50: )
    51: 
    52: from app.interfaces.provider_management.operations_center_dashboard_router import (
    53:     router as operations_center_dashboard_router,
    54: )
    55: 
    56: from app.api.cybersecurity_intelligence import (
    57:     router as cybersecurity_intelligence_router,
    58: )
    59: 
    60: 
    61: @asynccontextmanager
    62: async def lifespan(
    63:     application: FastAPI,
    64: ):
    65:     """
    66:     Manage application startup and shutdown lifecycle.
    67: 
    68:     Startup:
    69:         - compose application dependencies;
    70:         - create the authoritative functionality-control service;
    71:         - start Telegram transport runtime.
    72: 
    73:     Shutdown:
    74:         - stop Telegram transport runtime;
    75:         - dispose of the functionality-control database engine.
    76: 
    77:     This layer does not:
    78: 
    79:     - execute agents;
    80:     - execute capabilities;
    81:     - create providers;
    82:     - create Runtime components.
    83:     """
    84: 
    85:     bootstrap_application()
    86: 
    87:     (
    88:         functionality_control_service,
    89:         functionality_control_engine,
    90:     ) = create_standalone_functionality_control_service()
    91: 
    92:     application.state.functionality_control_service = (
    93:         functionality_control_service
    94:     )
    95:     application.state.functionality_control_engine = (
    96:         functionality_control_engine
    97:     )
    98: 
    99:     try:
   100:         await start_telegram_runtime(
   101:             functionality_control_service=(
   102:                 functionality_control_service
   103:             ),
   104:         )
   105: 
   106:         yield
   107: 
   108:     finally:
   109:         await stop_telegram_runtime()
   110: 
   111:         functionality_control_engine.dispose()
   112: 
   113:         application.state.functionality_control_service = None
   114:         application.state.functionality_control_engine = None
   115: 
   116: 
   117: def create_app() -> FastAPI:
   118:     """
   119:     Create the EasyChatBot FastAPI application.
   120: 
   121:     Application composition is intentionally separated
   122:     from the HTTP layer.
   123: 
   124:     This function:
   125: 
   126:     - creates the FastAPI application;
   127:     - configures local frontend access;
   128:     - registers API routes;
   129:     - attaches lifecycle management.
   130: 
   131:     It does not:
   132: 
   133:     - execute agents;
   134:     - execute capabilities;
   135:     - call providers directly;
   136:     - create Runtime executors.
   137:     """
   138: 
   139:     application = FastAPI(
   140:         title="EasyChatBot",
   141:         description="AI companion learning platform",
   142:         lifespan=lifespan,
   143:     )
   144: 
   145:     # =========================================================
   146:     # Local Frontend CORS
   147:     # =========================================================
   148: 
   149:     application.add_middleware(
   150:         CORSMiddleware,
   151:         allow_origins=[
   152:             "http://127.0.0.1:5173",
   153:             "http://localhost:5173",
   154:             "http://127.0.0.1:5500",
   155:             "http://localhost:5500",
   156:             "http://127.0.0.1:5501",
   157:             "http://localhost:5501",
   158:             "http://localhost:3000",
   159:             "http://127.0.0.1:3000",
   160:         ],
   161:         allow_credentials=True,
   162:         allow_methods=["*"],
   163:         allow_headers=["*"],
   164:     )
   165: 
   166:     # =========================================================
   167:     # Existing API Routes
   168:     # =========================================================
   169: 
   170:     application.include_router(
   171:         router,
   172:     )
   173: 
   174:     # =========================================================
   175:     # Agentic Cinema Routes
   176:     # =========================================================
   177:     #
   178:     # Browser
   179:     #     ↓
   180:     # /api/cinema
   181:     #     ↓
   182:     # FastAPI Cinema Boundary
   183:     #     ↓
   184:     # Google Agent Runtime
   185:     #     ↓
   186:     # Gemini + Parallel + G5 workflow
   187:     #
   188:     # The API layer does not execute the agent directly.
   189:     # The Cinema router owns the HTTP/application boundary.
   190:     # =========================================================
   191: 
   192:     application.include_router(
   193:         cinema_router,
   194:     )
   195: 
   196:     # =========================================================
   197:     # Character Theme Routes
   198:     # =========================================================
   199: 
   200:     application.include_router(
   201:         character_theme_router,
   202:     )
   203: 
   204:     # =========================================================
   205:     # Character Catalogue Routes
   206:     # =========================================================
   207: 
   208:     application.include_router(
   209:         character_router,
   210:     )
   211: 
   212:     # =========================================================
   213:     # AI Operations Center Routes
   214:     # =========================================================
   215: 
   216:     application.include_router(
   217:         operations_center_dashboard_router,
   218:     )
   219: 
   220:     # =========================================================
   221:     # Cybersecurity Intelligence Routes
   222:     # =========================================================
   223: 
   224:     application.include_router(
   225:         cybersecurity_intelligence_router,
   226:     )
   227: 
   228:     return application
   229: 
   230: 
   231: app = create_app()
   232: 
   233: 
   234: @app.get("/")
   235: def health() -> dict[str, str]:
   236:     """
   237:     Return application health status.
   238:     """
   239: 
   240:     return {
   241:         "status": "EasyChatBot running",
   242:         "architecture": (
   243:             "Runtime + Agents + Memory"
   244:         ),
   245:     }

     1: """
     2: EasyChatBot API Routes.
     3: 
     4: Application HTTP boundary.
     5: 
     6: Responsibilities:
     7: 
     8: - expose HTTP endpoints;
     9: - validate transport input;
    10: - delegate to application services.
    11: 
    12: This module does not:
    13: 
    14: - compose Runtime;
    15: - create providers;
    16: - execute agents directly;
    17: - implement capability logic.

    19: Architecture:
    20:

    23:  v

   273: # =========================================================
   274: # Runtime Service Configuration
   275: # =========================================================
   276: 
   277: _external_runtime_service_override: (
   278:     ExternalRuntimeService | None
   279: ) = None
   280: 
   281: 
   282: def configure_external_runtime_service(
   283:     service: ExternalRuntimeService,
   284: ) -> None:
   285:     """
   286:     Configure an explicit Runtime service override.
   287: 
   288:     Primarily used by application tests and controlled
   289:     application composition.
   290: 
   291:     Production Runtime ownership remains with:
   292: 
   293:         backend.runtime.external_runtime_container
   294:     """
   295: 
   296:     global _external_runtime_service_override
   297: 
   298:     if service is None:
   299:         raise ValueError(
   300:             "ExternalRuntimeService cannot be None."
   301:         )
   302: 
   303:     _external_runtime_service_override = service
   304: 
   305: 
   306: def create_external_runtime_service() -> ExternalRuntimeService:
   307:     """
   308:     Retrieve the configured Runtime service.
   309: 
   310:     Resolution order:
   311: 
   312:     1. Explicit test/application override.
   313:     2. Runtime container.
   314:     3. Controlled in-memory Runtime fallback.
   315: 
   316:     The fallback exists only so API tests can execute
   317:     without requiring full application bootstrap.
   318:     """
   319: 
   320:     if (
   321:         _external_runtime_service_override
   322:         is not None
   323:     ):
   324:         return _external_runtime_service_override
   325: 
   326:     try:
   327:         return get_external_runtime_service()
   328: 
   329:     except RuntimeError:
   330:         gateway = create_external_runtime_gateway(
   331:             live_intelligence_agent=(
   332:                 TestLiveIntelligenceAgent()
   333:             ),
   334:             podcast_discovery_agent=(
   335:                 _create_test_podcast_agent()
   336:             ),
   337:             podcast_providers=[
   338:                 _create_test_podcast_provider()
   339:             ],
   340:             governance=(
   341:                 _create_test_governance()
   342:             ),
   343:         )
   344: 
   345:         return (
   346:             application_factory
   347:             .create_external_runtime_service(
   348:                 gateway=gateway,
   349:             )
   350:         )
   351: 
   352: 
   353: def create_runtime_application_service(
   354: ) -> RuntimeApplicationService:
   355:     """
   356:     Create the application-layer Runtime service.
   357: 
   358:     API routes remain unaware of Runtime composition.
   359:     """
   360: 
   361:     return (
   362:         application_factory
   363:         .create_runtime_application_service(
   364:             runtime_service=(
   365:                 create_external_runtime_service()
   366:             ),
   367:         )
   368:     )
   369: 
   370:

   388: def create_companion_service(

   393:     """

   409: # Character Endpoint

   412: 
   413: @router.get("/characters")
   414: def get_characters():
   415:     """
   416:     Return the available learner characters.
   417:     """
   418:

   421:     )
   422:

   425:             {

   428:                 "description": character.description,
   429:                 "personality": character.personality,

   435:         ]
   436:     }

   439: # =========================================================
   440: # Chat Endpoint
   441: # =========================================================
   442: 
   443: 
   444: @router.post(
   445:     "/chat",
   446:     response_model=ChatResponse,
   447: )
   448: async def chat(
   449:     payload: ChatRequest,
   450: ) -> ChatResponse:
   451:     """
   452:     Execute a Companion conversation.
   453:     """
   454: 
   455:     if (
   456:         payload.message is None
   457:         or not payload.message.strip()
   458:     ):
   459:         raise HTTPException(
   460:             status_code=400,
   461:             detail="message is required",
   462:         )
   463: 
   464:     try:
   465:         character = character_registry.get(
   466:             payload.character,
   467:         )
   468: 
   469:     except ValueError as exc:
   470:         raise HTTPException(
   471:             status_code=400,
   472:             detail=str(exc),
   473:         ) from exc
   474: 
   475:     service = create_companion_service(
   476:         character,
   477:     )
   478: 
   479:     context = ExecutionContext(
   480:         user_id=payload.user_id,
   481:         request=payload.message,
   482:     )
   483: 
   484:     result = await service.interact(
   485:         context,
   486:         use_research=payload.research,
   487:     )
   488: 
   489:     return ChatResponse(
   490:         character=result["character"],
   491:         conversation=result["conversation"],
   492:         research=result["research"],
   493:         quality=result["quality"],
   494:         memory_count=result["memory_count"],
   495:         memory_context_count=result.get(
   496:             "memory_context_count",
   497:             0,
   498:         ),
   499:     )
   500: 
   501: 
   502: # =========================================================
   503: # Runtime Execute Endpoint
   504: # =========================================================
   505: 
   506: 
   507: @router.post(
   508:     "/runtime/execute",
   509:     response_model=RuntimeExecuteResponse,
   510: )
   511: async def execute_runtime(
   512:     payload: RuntimeExecuteRequest,
   513:     principal: Principal = Depends(get_current_principal),
   514: ) -> RuntimeExecuteResponse:
   515:     """
   516:     Execute an approved Runtime capability.
   517: 
   518:     The API boundary authenticates the caller and converts the
   519:     application Principal into the provider-independent
   520:     RuntimeActorContext.
   521: 
   522:     HTTP concerns remain here.
   523: 
   524:     Runtime orchestration remains in:
   525: 
   526:         RuntimeApplicationService
   527:     """
   528: 
   529:     actor = RuntimeActorContext(
   530:         actor_id=principal.user_id,
   531:         roles=principal.roles,
   532:     )
   533: 
   534:     service = create_runtime_application_service()
   535: 
   536:     try:
   537:         result = await service.execute(
   538:             capability=payload.capability,
   539:             payload=payload.payload,
   540:             actor=actor,
   541:         )
   542: 
   543:     except ValueError as exc:
   544:         raise HTTPException(
   545:             status_code=400,
   546:             detail=str(exc),
   547:         ) from exc
   548: 
   549:     except Exception as exc:
   550:         raise HTTPException(
   551:             status_code=500,
   552:             detail=str(exc),
   553:         ) from exc
   554: 
   555:     if isinstance(result, dict):
   556:         return RuntimeExecuteResponse(
   557:             request_id=result["request_id"],
   558:             capability=result["capability"],
   559:             status=result["status"],
   560:             output=result.get("output"),
   561:             error=result.get("error"),
   562:         )
   563: 
   564:     return RuntimeExecuteResponse(
   565:         request_id=result.request_id,
   566:         capability=result.capability,
   567:         status=result.status,
   568:         output=result.output,
   569:         error=getattr(
   570:             result,
   571:             "error",
   572:             None,
   573:         ),
   574:     )
   575: 
   576: 
   577: # =========================================================
   578: # Podcast Discovery Endpoint
   579: # =========================================================
   580: 
   581: 
   582: @router.post(
   583:     "/capabilities/podcast/discover",
   584: )
   585: async def discover_podcast(
   586:     payload: dict,
   587: ):
   588:     """
   589:     Execute Podcast Discovery through the Runtime.
   590: 
   591:     The endpoint only prepares transport-level defaults.
   592:     Discovery remains owned by the Runtime capability.
   593:     """
   594: 
   595:     if not payload.get("query"):
   596:         raise HTTPException(
   597:             status_code=400,
   598:             detail="query is required",
   599:         )
   600: 
   601:     service = create_runtime_application_service()
   602: 
   603:     enriched_payload = {
   604:         "user_id": payload.get(
   605:             "user_id",
   606:             "demo-user",
   607:         ),
   608:         "character_id": payload.get(
   609:             "character_id",
   610:             "yeti",
   611:         ),
   612:         "character_name": payload.get(
   613:             "character_name",
   614:             "Yeti",
   615:         ),
   616:         "character_message": payload.get(
   617:             "character_message",
   618:             (
   619:                 "Help me discover useful "
   620:                 "learning content."
   621:             ),
   622:         ),
   623:         **payload,
   624:     }
   625: 
   626:     try:
   627:         result = await service.execute(
   628:             capability="podcast_discovery",
   629:             payload=enriched_payload,
   630:         )
   631: 
   632:     except ValueError as exc:
   633:         raise HTTPException(
   634:             status_code=400,
   635:             detail=str(exc),
   636:         ) from exc
   637: 
   638:     except Exception as exc:
   639:         raise HTTPException(
   640:             status_code=500,
   641:             detail=str(exc),
   642:         ) from exc
   643: 
   644:     if isinstance(result, dict):
   645:         return {
   646:             "request_id": result["request_id"],
   647:             "capability": result["capability"],
   648:             "status": result["status"],
   649:             "output": result.get("output"),
   650:         }
   651: 
   652:     return {
   653:         "request_id": result.request_id,
   654:         "capability": result.capability,
   655:         "status": result.status,
   656:         "output": result.output,
   657:     }
   658: 
   659: 
   660: # =========================================================
   661: # Hackathon Demo Endpoint
   662: # =========================================================
   663: 
   664: 
   665: @router.post(
   666:     "/hackathon/demo",
   667:     response_model=HackathonDemoResponse,
   668: )
   669: async def hackathon_demo(
   670:     payload: HackathonDemoRequest,
   671: ) -> HackathonDemoResponse:
   672:     """
   673:     Hackathon Runtime vertical slice.
   674: 
   675:     API
   676:      |
   677:      v
   678:     RuntimeApplicationService
   679:      |
   680:      v
   681:     ExternalRuntimeService
   682:      |
   683:      v
   684:     CapabilityGateway
   685:      |
   686:      v
   687:     Live Intelligence Executor
   688:      |
   689:      v
   690:     LiveIntelligenceAgent
   691:     """
   692: 
   693:     service = create_runtime_application_service()
   694: 
   695:     try:
   696:         result = await service.execute(
   697:             capability="live_intelligence",
   698:             payload={
   699:                 "user_id": payload.user_id,
   700:                 "topic": payload.message,
   701:                 "language": "French",
   702:                 "learner_level": "B2",
   703:             },
   704:         )
   705: 
   706:     except ValueError as exc:
   707:         raise HTTPException(
   708:             status_code=400,
   709:             detail=str(exc),
   710:         ) from exc
   711: 
   712:     except Exception as exc:
   713:         raise HTTPException(
   714:             status_code=500,
   715:             detail=str(exc),
   716:         ) from exc
   717: 
   718:     if isinstance(result, dict):
   719:         return HackathonDemoResponse(
   720:             status=result["status"],
   721:             capability=result["capability"],
   722:             output=result.get("output"),
   723:             request_id=result["request_id"],
   724:         )
   725: 
   726:     return HackathonDemoResponse(
   727:         status=result.status,
   728:         capability=result.capability,
   729:         output=result.output,
   730:         request_id=result.request_id,
   731:     )
   732: 
   733: 
   734: # =========================================================
   735: # Hackathon Flash Deal Card Endpoint
   736: # =========================================================
   737: 
   738: 
   739: @router.get(
   740:     "/hackathon/flash-deal",
   741:     response_model=FlashDealResponse,
   742: )
   743: def hackathon_flash_deal() -> FlashDealResponse:
   744:     """
   745:     Return the deterministic hackathon Flash Deal Card.
   746: 
   747:     Flow:
   748: 
   749:     Demo Deal
   750:         |
   751:         v
   752:     Demo Memory
   753:         |
   754:         v
   755:     DiscoveryRequest
   756:         |
   757:         v
   758:     DiscoveryService
   759:         |
   760:         v
   761:     FlashDealCard
   762:         |
   763:         v
   764:     HTTP Response
   765: 
   766:     This endpoint is intentionally deterministic for the
   767:     hackathon frontend integration.
   768: 
   769:     It does not:
   770: 
   771:     - perform external discovery;
   772:     - call provider SDKs;
   773:     - process payments;
   774:     - create purchases;
   775:     - bypass Runtime controls;
   776:     - invent product data outside the demo builder.
   777: 
   778:     The existing FlashDealCard remains the application-facing
   779:     presentation contract. This route only adapts it to HTTP.
   780:     """
   781: 
   782:     deal = build_demo_deal()
   783: 
   784:     memory = build_demo_memory()
   785: 
   786:     request = DiscoveryRequest(
   787:         user_id=memory.user_id,
   788:         deal=deal,
   789:         character=CharacterContext(
   790:             character_id="yeti",
   791:             character_name="Yeti",
   792:             message=(
   793:                 "Look what I found! "
   794:                 "This could be perfect for you."
   795:             ),
   796:         ),
   797:         product_image_url=(
   798:             "https://example.com/demo/iphone17-pro.jpg"
   799:         ),
   800:         tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
   801:         highlights=(
   802:             "Pro camera system",
   803:             "High-performance chip",
   804:             "All-day battery life",
   805:             "Premium display",
   806:         ),
   807:     )
   808: 
   809:     card = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
   810:         request,
   811:         memories=[memory],
   812:     )
   813: 
   814:     return FlashDealResponse(
   815:         deal_id=card.deal_id,
   816: 
   817:         character=FlashDealCharacterResponse(
   818:             id=card.character_id,
   819:             name=card.character_name,
   820:             message=card.character_message,
   821:         ),
   822: 
   823:         product=FlashDealProductResponse(
   824:             name=card.product_name,
   825:             image_url=card.product_image_url,
   826:             tagline=card.tagline,
   827:             highlights=list(card.highlights),
   828:         ),
   829: 
   830:         pricing=FlashDealPricingResponse(
   831:             current_price=card.current_price,
   832:             currency=card.currency,
   833:             previous_price=card.previous_price,
   834:             discount_percentage=(
   835:                 card.discount_percentage
   836:             ),
   837:             savings=card.savings,
   838:         ),
   839: 
   840:         relevance_reason=(
   841:             card.relevance_reason
   842:         ),
   843: 
   844:         vendor=card.vendor_name,
   845: 
   846:         actions=FlashDealActionsResponse(
   847:             get_deal=card.get_deal_url,
   848:             more_information=(
   849:                 card.more_information_url
   850:             ),
   851:         ),
   852:     )
   853:

    67:         ),

   200: # =========================================================
   201: # Governance
   202: # =========================================================
   203: 
   204: 
   205: class BootstrapGovernanceDecision:
   206:     """
   207:     Runtime governance decision.
   208:     """
   209: 
   210:     def __init__(
   211:         self,
   212:         allowed: bool,
   213:     ) -> None:
   214: 
   215:         self.allowed = allowed
   216: 
   217: 
   218: class BootstrapGovernance:
   219:     """
   220:     Temporary hackathon governance implementation.
   221: 
   222:     Sensitive internal-data capabilities fail closed.
   223: 
   224:     The Runtime receives a provider-independent
   225:     RuntimeActorContext. Application/API identity remains
   226:     outside the Runtime boundary.
   227:     """
   228: 
   229:     def evaluate(
   230:         self,
   231:         request,
   232:     ) -> BootstrapGovernanceDecision:
   233: 
   234:         capability = getattr(
   235:             request,
   236:             "capability",
   237:             None,
   238:         )
   239: 
   240:         capability_value = getattr(
   241:             capability,
   242:             "value",
   243:             capability,
   244:         )
   245: 
   246:         if capability_value == "internal_data_read":
   247:             actor = getattr(
   248:                 request,
   249:                 "actor",
   250:                 None,
   251:             )
   252: 
   253:             if not isinstance(
   254:                 actor,
   255:                 RuntimeActorContext,
   256:             ):
   257:                 return BootstrapGovernanceDecision(
   258:                     allowed=False,
   259:                 )
   260: 
   261:             allowed = has_permission(
   262:                 roles=actor.roles,
   263:                 permission=Permission.INTERNAL_DATA_READ,
   264:             )
   265: 
   266:             return BootstrapGovernanceDecision(
   267:                 allowed=allowed,
   268:             )
   269: 
   270:         return BootstrapGovernanceDecision(
   271:             allowed=True,
   272:         )
   273: 
   274: 
   275: def create_governance():
   276:     """
   277:     Create Runtime governance dependency.
   278:     """
   279: 
   280:     return BootstrapGovernance()
   281: 

####################################################################
FILE: backend/application/factory.py
####################################################################
     1: """
     2: Application Factory.
     3: 
     4: Creates application-layer services around the authoritative
     5: external Runtime composition.
     6: 
     7: The factory delegates Runtime capability composition to
     8: ``src.ai_runtime.composition`` and wraps the resulting gateway
     9: with application-layer services.
    10: 
    11: It does not create a second Runtime, registry, executor,
    12: provider abstraction, or execution engine.
    13: """
    14: 
    15: from __future__ import annotations
    16: 
    17: from typing import Any, Sequence
    18: 
    19: from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.gateway import CapabilityGateway
    20: from src.ai_runtime.composition import (
    21:     create_external_runtime_gateway,
    22: )
    23: 
    24: from services.external_runtime_service import (
    25:     ExternalRuntimeService,
    26: )
    27: from services.runtime.runtime_application_service import (
    28:     RuntimeApplicationService,
    29: )
    30: 
    31: 
    32: class ApplicationFactory:
    33:     """Application composition factory."""
    34: 
    35:     @staticmethod
    36:     def create_external_runtime(
    37:         *,
    38:         live_intelligence_agent: Any,
    39:         podcast_discovery_agent: Any,
    40:         podcast_providers: Sequence[Any],
    41:         governance: Any,
    42:         internal_documentation_agent: Any | None = None,
    43:     ) -> ExternalRuntimeService:
    44:         """
    45:         Compose the authoritative external Runtime gateway and
    46:         expose it through the application-facing service.
    47: 
    48:         Runtime internals remain owned by
    49:         ``src.ai_runtime.composition``.
    50: 
    51:         This method does not create a parallel Runtime architecture.
    52:         """
    53: 
    54:         gateway = create_external_runtime_gateway(
    55:             live_intelligence_agent=live_intelligence_agent,
    56:             podcast_discovery_agent=podcast_discovery_agent,
    57:             podcast_providers=podcast_providers,
    58:             governance=governance,
    59:             internal_documentation_agent=(
    60:                 internal_documentation_agent
    61:             ),
    62:         )
    63: 
    64:         return ApplicationFactory.create_external_runtime_service(
    65:             gateway=gateway,
    66:         )
    67: 
    68:     @staticmethod
    69:     def create_external_runtime_service(
    70:         *,
    71:         gateway: CapabilityGateway,
    72:     ) -> ExternalRuntimeService:
    73:         """
    74:         Create the application-facing ExternalRuntimeService
    75:         around an already-composed CapabilityGateway.
    76:         """
    77: 
    78:         if gateway is None:
    79:             raise ValueError(
    80:                 "CapabilityGateway cannot be None"
    81:             )
    82: 
    83:         return ExternalRuntimeService(
    84:             gateway=gateway,
    85:         )
    86: 
    87:     @staticmethod
    88:     def create_runtime_application_service(
    89:         *,
    90:         runtime_service: ExternalRuntimeService,
    91:     ) -> RuntimeApplicationService:
    92:         """
    93:         Create the application RuntimeApplicationService.
    94: 
    95:         The RuntimeApplicationService receives an already-composed
    96:         ExternalRuntimeService.
    97: 
    98:         This factory does not:
    99: 
   100:         - create providers;
   101:         - create agents;
   102:         - create gateways;
   103:         - compose Runtime internals;
   104:         - execute capabilities.
   105:         """
   106: 
   107:         if runtime_service is None:
   108:             raise ValueError(
   109:                 "ExternalRuntimeService cannot be None"
   110:             )
   111: 
   112:         return RuntimeApplicationService(
   113:             runtime_service=runtime_service,
   114:         )
   115:

   167: def _required_environment(name: str) -> str:
   168:     value = os.getenv(name, "").strip()
   169: 
   170:     if not value:
   171:         raise RuntimeError(
   172:             f"{name} is required for authentication email composition."
   173:         )

   178: def _create_announcement_components():
   179:     telegram_client = TelegramAnnouncementClient()
   180: 
   181:     announcement_publisher = TelegramAnnouncementPublisher(
   182:         telegram_client=telegram_client,
   183:     )
   184: 
   185:     return (
   186:         telegram_client,
   187:         announcement_publisher,
   188:     )

   190: 
   191: @asynccontextmanager
   192: async def lifespan(
   193:     application: FastAPI,
   194: ):
   195:     scheduler_runtime = application.state.scheduler_runtime
   196: 
   197:     try:
   198:         await scheduler_runtime.start()
   199:     except Exception as exc:
   200:         print(
   201:             "Cybersecurity scheduler startup failed:",
   202:             exc,
   203:         )
   204: 
   205:     yield
   206: 
   207:     try:
   208:         await scheduler_runtime.stop()
   209:     except Exception as exc:
   210:         print(
   211:             "Cybersecurity scheduler shutdown failed:",
   212:             exc,
   213:         )
   214:     finally:
   215:         telegram_client = getattr(
   216:             application.state,
   217:             "announcement_telegram_client",
   218:             None,
   219:         )
   220: 
   221:         if telegram_client is not None:
   222:             try:
   223:                 await telegram_client.close()
   224:             except Exception as exc:
   225:                 print(
   226:                     "Telegram announcement client shutdown failed:",
   227:                     exc,
   228:                 )
   229: 
   230:         auth_engine = getattr(
   231:             application.state,
   232:             "auth_engine",
   233:             None,
   234:         )
   235: 
   236:         if auth_engine is not None:
   237:             try:
   238:                 await auth_engine.dispose()
   239:             except Exception as exc:
   240:                 print(
   241:                     "PostgreSQL authentication engine shutdown failed:",
